```java
    /** One campaign; returns the continued state (never a restart). */
    public State campaign(State s, Eras e, int generations, int population, int hofSize) {
        devFit.clear(); valFit.clear();                                  // eras moved: everything is re-scored
        curEras = e;                                                     // credit-guided children read weights from draws < valEnd only
        curState = s;
        if (s.population.isEmpty()) {
            for (ModelGenomeSU2 g : screenSeeds(r14)) s.population.add(new Ranked(g, "screen", "", ""));
            if (r14b) {
                for (Object[] gc : new Object[][]{{"MASS", 0.15}, {"MASS", 0.30}, {"META", 0.15}})
                    s.population.add(new Ranked(new ModelGenomeSU2(Collections.singletonList(defaultAtomNet()), "STATIC", false, (String) gc[0], (Double) gc[1], 0.2, true), "screen", "", ""));
                List<Integer> a = new ArrayList<>(); for (int x : defaultAtoms()) a.add(x);
                s.population.add(new Ranked(new ModelGenomeSU2(Collections.singletonList(atomNet(a, new ArrayList<>(), Collections.singletonList(1), true, 0.05, 1e-4, 0.999)), "STATIC", false, "MASS", 0.15, 0.2, true), "screen", "", ""));
            }
            if (r16)                                                      // regime-memory families at their central parameters
                for (String kind : ModelGenomeSU2.R16_KINDS) for (String gate : new String[]{"MASS", "META"}) for (double cov : new double[]{0.15, 0.30})
                    s.population.add(new Ranked(new ModelGenomeSU2(Collections.singletonList(new ModelGenomeSU2.Member(kind, centralParams(kind), 1.0)), "STATIC", false, gate, cov, 0.2, true), "screen:" + kind, "", ""));
            if (r15) {                                                    // the user's XI method decomposed into its atoms
                ModelGenomeSU2.Member xi = atomNet(xiAtoms(), new ArrayList<>(), new ArrayList<>(), false, 0.02, 1e-4, 1.0);
                for (Object[] gc : new Object[][]{{"MASS", 0.15}, {"MASS", 0.30}, {"META", 0.15}})
                    s.population.add(new Ranked(new ModelGenomeSU2(Collections.singletonList(xi), "STATIC", false, (String) gc[0], (Double) gc[1], 0.2, true), "screen:XI_DECOMPOSED", "", ""));
            }
        }
        String[] kinds = kinds();
        while (s.population.size() < population) s.population.add(new Ranked(randomGenome(kinds[s.population.size() % kinds.length]), "random", "", ""));
        double champVal = s.champion == null ? -9 : val(s.champion.g, e);
        Map<String, Double> tierVal = new TreeMap<>(); for (Map.Entry<String, Ranked> t : s.tiers.entrySet()) tierVal.put(t.getKey(), val(t.getValue().g, e));
        int evaluated = 0;
        for (int gen = 0; gen < generations; gen++) {
            if (r14) {                                                    // consensus of the central models and the hall of fame
                List<ModelGenomeSU2> progs = centralPrograms(r14b, r16);
                Map<String, Integer> perFamily = new HashMap<>();
                for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(hofSize, s.hallOfFame.size()); i++) {
                    ModelGenomeSU2 h = s.hallOfFame.get(i).g;
                    if (r14b) {                                           // R14b: at most 2 programs per family signature
                        TreeSet<String> fam = new TreeSet<>(); for (ModelGenomeSU2.Member m : h.members) fam.add(m.kind);
                        int c0 = perFamily.merge(String.join("+", fam), 1, Integer::sum); if (c0 > 2) continue;
                    }
                    progs.add(h);
                }
                buildConsensus(progs); devFit.clear(); valFit.clear();
            }
            List<Ranked> scored = new ArrayList<>(s.population);
            scored.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble((Ranked r) -> -dev(r.g, e)).thenComparing(r -> r.g.key()));
            Set<String> inHof = new HashSet<>(); for (Ranked h : s.hallOfFame) inHof.add(h.g.key());
            for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(6, scored.size()); i++) if (inHof.add(scored.get(i).g.key())) s.hallOfFame.add(scored.get(i));
            s.hallOfFame.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble((Ranked r) -> -dev(r.g, e)).thenComparing(r -> r.g.key()));
            while (s.hallOfFame.size() > hofSize) s.hallOfFame.remove(s.hallOfFame.size() - 1);
            if (r15) updatePrior(s);
            LinkedHashMap<String, Ranked> cands = new LinkedHashMap<>();
            for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(8, scored.size()); i++) cands.putIfAbsent(scored.get(i).g.key(), scored.get(i));
            for (String t : TIERS) { int k = 0; for (Ranked r : scored) if (tierOf(r.g).equals(t) && k++ < 3) cands.putIfAbsent(r.g.key(), r); }
            Ranked bestC = null; double bestV = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
            for (Ranked r : cands.values()) {
                double v = val(r.g, e);
                if (v > bestV) { bestV = v; bestC = r; }
                String t = tierOf(r.g); if (v > tierVal.getOrDefault(t, -9.0)) { tierVal.put(t, v); s.tiers.put(t, r); }
            }
            if (r14) {
                // committees per tier: the top programs by the judge at that tier's frequency, distinct tables only
                LinkedHashMap<String, Ranked> pool = new LinkedHashMap<>();
                for (int i = 0; i < Math.min(30, scored.size()); i++) pool.putIfAbsent(scored.get(i).g.key(), scored.get(i));
                for (Ranked h : s.hallOfFame) pool.putIfAbsent(h.g.key(), h);
                for (String t : TIERS) {
                    double tc = tierCoverage(t); List<Object[]> ranked = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (Ranked r : pool.values()) { ModelGenomeSU2 a = r.g.asTier(tc); ranked.add(new Object[]{val(a, e), a}); }
                    ranked.sort((x, y) -> { int c0 = Double.compare((Double) y[0], (Double) x[0]); return c0 != 0 ? c0 : ((ModelGenomeSU2) x[1]).key().compareTo(((ModelGenomeSU2) y[1]).key()); });
                    List<ModelGenomeSU2> subs = new ArrayList<>(); Set<String> tk = new HashSet<>();
                    for (Object[] x : ranked) { ModelGenomeSU2 a = (ModelGenomeSU2) x[1]; if (tk.add(tableKey(a)) && !subsHasText(subs, a)) subs.add(a); if (subs.size() == CommitteeSU2.MAX_MEMBERS) break; }
                    if (!subs.isEmpty()) s.committees.put(t, new CommitteeSU2(subs, tc));
                }
                double cj = s.committees.containsKey("F15") ? judge(CommitteeSU2.evaluate(s.committees.get("F15"), lib, startMeta), e.devStart, e.valEnd, 4) : -9;
                if (cj > s.committeeJudge) { s.committeeJudge = cj; s.effectiveGenerations++; }
            }
            if (bestV > champVal) { champVal = bestV; s.champion = bestC; if (!r14) s.effectiveGenerations++; }
            int eliteN = Math.max(4, population / 4);
            List<Ranked> next = new ArrayList<>(scored.subList(0, Math.min(eliteN, scored.size())));
            List<Ranked> parents = new ArrayList<>(next); parents.addAll(s.hallOfFame);
            Set<String> keys = new HashSet<>(); for (Ranked r : next) keys.add(r.g.key());
            while (next.size() < population) {
                String op = pickOp(s); Ranked parent;
                if (rng.nextDouble() < .2) { parent = new Ranked(randomGenome(pickKind(s, evaluated)), "random", "", ""); op = "mutate"; }
                else parent = parents.get(rng.nextInt(parents.size()));
                Ranked child = apply(op, parent, s.hallOfFame, e);
                if (!keys.add(child.g.key())) { child = apply("mutate", child, s.hallOfFame, e); keys.add(child.g.key()); }
                double pf = dev(parent.g, e), cf = dev(child.g, e); evaluated++;
                double[] os = s.opStats.computeIfAbsent(op, k -> new double[]{1, 1}); os[0]++; if (cf > pf) os[1]++;
                for (ModelGenomeSU2.Member m : child.g.members) { double[] ks = s.kindStats.computeIfAbsent(m.kind, k -> new double[]{1, 0}); ks[0]++; ks[1] += Math.max(0, cf - pf); }
                if (!child.note.isEmpty() && s.repairNotes.size() < 200) s.repairNotes.add(child.note + "|parentDev=" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", pf) + "|childDev=" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", cf));
                next.add(child);
            }
            s.population.clear(); s.population.addAll(next);
        }
        return s;
    }
```

#### P21 结构变异·交叉·融合·去除无效复杂度

- 优先级 **P1**；依赖 P20；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U09, U11；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P022, P023

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 旧进化以调参为主；复杂度增加了却不贡献（旧 VM 大量死指令）。

**做什么（目标）**

11 种算子：mutate、add、remove、reweight、pool、gate、coverage、crossover、fusion（从名人堂借成员）、diagnose（见 P22）、prune（删去对 DEV 无贡献的成员）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. ModelEvolutionSU2.apply；成员按 (种类,参数) 去重；算子成功率被记住并用于抽样。

**文件：1 个（新增 1 / 修改 0 / 文档 0 / 检查 0；删除 0 / 整文件重写 0）**

| 文件 | 动作 | 说明 | BEFORE | AFTER |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` | ADD 新增 | 算子 | —（新文件） | 664 行 · sha256 c98e451e00ffef3b… |

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` 第 507–554 行：

```java
    Ranked apply(String op, Ranked parentR, List<Ranked> hof, Eras e) {
        if (op.equals("distill") && r15) { Ranked c = distillChild(parentR, e); if (c != null) return c; op = "mutate"; }
        ModelGenomeSU2 g = parentR.g; List<ModelGenomeSU2.Member> mem = new ArrayList<>(g.members);
        String pool = g.pool, gate = g.gate; boolean sink = g.sink; double cov = g.coverage, c = g.metaC; String note = "";
        switch (op) {
            case "credit": {
                int ai = -1; for (int i = 0; i < mem.size(); i++) if (mem.get(i).kind.equals("ATOMNET")) ai = i;
                if (ai >= 0) { mem.set(ai, creditChild(mem.get(ai))); note = lastNote; break; }
                if (mem.size() < 4) { mem.add(defaultAtomNet()); note = "PARENT_HAS_NO_ATOM_NETWORK|CHILD_GRAFTS_DEFAULT_ATOMNET"; break; }
                op = "mutate";
            } // fallthrough
            case "add": if (mem.size() < 4 && !op.equals("mutate")) { mem.add(randomMember(randomKind())); break; } op = "mutate"; // fallthrough
            case "mutate": { int i = rng.nextInt(mem.size()); lastNote = ""; mem.set(i, jitter(mem.get(i))); note = lastNote; break; }
            case "remove": if (mem.size() > 1) mem.remove(rng.nextInt(mem.size())); else { int i = rng.nextInt(mem.size()); mem.set(i, jitter(mem.get(i))); } break;
            case "reweight": if (mem.size() > 1) { int i = rng.nextInt(mem.size()); ModelGenomeSU2.Member x = mem.get(i); double w = Math.max(0.1, Math.min(3.0, x.weight * Math.exp(rng.nextGaussian() * 0.4))); mem.set(i, new ModelGenomeSU2.Member(x.kind, x.params, Math.round(w * 100) / 100.0)); } break;
            case "pool": { String np; do { np = randomPool(); } while (np.equals(pool)); pool = np; if (rng.nextDouble() < .4) sink = !sink;
                if (TeacherPoolSU2.isTeacher(pool)) note = "CHILD_USES_TEACHER_MATRIX_BY_STATE_" + AtomStreamSU2.STATES[TeacherPoolSU2.stateOf(pool)].name(); break; }
            case "gate": if (gate.equals("MASS")) gate = "META"; else c = new double[]{0.05, 0.1, 0.2, 0.5}[rng.nextInt(4)]; break;
            case "coverage": { double nc; do { nc = randomCoverage(); } while (nc == cov); cov = nc; break; }
            case "crossover": if (!hof.isEmpty()) { Ranked o = hof.get(rng.nextInt(hof.size())); List<ModelGenomeSU2.Member> u = new ArrayList<>(g.members); u.addAll(o.g.members); Collections.shuffle(u, rng); int k = Math.max(1, Math.min(4, (g.members.size() + o.g.members.size() + 1) / 2)); mem = dedupe(u.subList(0, Math.min(k, u.size()))); } break;
            case "fusion": if (!hof.isEmpty() && mem.size() < 4) { Ranked o = hof.get(rng.nextInt(hof.size())); mem.add(o.g.members.get(rng.nextInt(o.g.members.size()))); pool = r15 ? randomPool() : new String[]{"STATIC", "BMA", "GATED"}[rng.nextInt(3)]; } break;
            case "diagnose": {
                String[] w = diagnose(g, e);
                if (w != null) {
                    boolean has = false; for (ModelGenomeSU2.Member m : mem) has |= m.kind.equals(w[0]);
                    if (!has && mem.size() < 4) mem.add(randomMember(w[0]));
                    gate = "META";
                    note = "PARENT_LOSES_WHEN_" + w[0] + "_STATE_" + w[1] + "|rate=" + w[2] + "|n=" + w[3] + "|gap=" + w[4] + "|CHILD_ADDS_" + w[0] + "_AND_APPLICABILITY_GATE";
                }
                break;
            }
            case "prune": {
                // effective-complexity audit: drop the member whose removal costs nothing on DEV
                if (mem.size() > 1) {
                    double base = dev(g, e), bestF = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY; int bestI = -1;
                    for (int i = 0; i < mem.size(); i++) {
                        List<ModelGenomeSU2.Member> less = new ArrayList<>(mem); less.remove(i);
                        double f = dev(new ModelGenomeSU2(less, pool, sink, gate, cov, c, g.calibrated), e);
                        if (f >= base - 1e-4 && f > bestF) { bestF = f; bestI = i; }
                    }
                    if (bestI >= 0) { note = "MEMBER_" + mem.get(bestI).kind + "_HAS_NO_DEV_CONTRIBUTION|without=" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", bestF) + "|with=" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", base) + "|CHILD_REMOVES_IT"; mem.remove(bestI); }
                }
                break;
            }
            default: { int i = rng.nextInt(mem.size()); mem.set(i, jitter(mem.get(i))); }
        }
        return new Ranked(new ModelGenomeSU2(dedupe(mem), pool, sink, gate, cov, c, g.calibrated), op, g.key(), note);
    }
```

#### P22 失败诊断→定向修复（父代的问题分析反馈给子代）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P20；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U12；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P019, P020

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 旧系统未中就“讲因果”，但不改变下一代；父代输在哪里没人知道。

**做什么（目标）**

对父代在 DEV 已结算行上按各状态量（变点风险、区制后验、记忆尺度、关系可靠度）三分位分组，找出它输得最多的状态；子代加入能感知该状态的成员并启用适用状态门，学会在那种状态下不出手。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. ModelEvolutionSU2.diagnose → apply("diagnose")；修复说明写入 su2_lineage.note 与界面，例如：PARENT_LOSES_WHEN_BOCPD_STATE_LOW|rate=0.3193|n=238|gap=-0.0164|CHILD_ADDS_BOCPD_AND_APPLICABILITY_GATE。

**文件：1 个（新增 1 / 修改 0 / 文档 0 / 检查 0；删除 0 / 整文件重写 0）**

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` | ADD 新增 | diagnose | —（新文件） | 664 行 · sha256 c98e451e00ffef3b… |

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` 第 481–505 行：

```java
    /** Feedback analysis (P22): the state in which the parent loses most, on DEV settled rows only. */
    String[] diagnose(ModelGenomeSU2 g, Eras e) {
        ModelReplaySU2.Result r = eval(g);
        int nAct = r.actions(e.devStart, e.devEnd);
        double base = nAct > 0 ? r.hits(e.devStart, e.devEnd) / (double) nAct : 0.3;
        double worstGap = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY; String[] worst = null;
        for (String kind : r16 ? new String[]{"BOCPD", "HMM", "MSDIR", "RELBAYES", "REGMEM", "RELMEM"} : new String[]{"BOCPD", "HMM", "MSDIR", "RELBAYES"}) {
            float[][] st = lib.get(new ModelGenomeSU2.Member(kind, centralParams(kind), 1.0)).state;
            int n = e.devEnd - e.devStart; double[] v = new double[n];
            for (int t = 0; t < n; t++) v[t] = st[e.devStart + t][0];
            double[] sorted = v.clone(); Arrays.sort(sorted);
            double q1 = ModelReplaySU2.quantileLinear(sorted.clone(), 1 / 3.0), q2 = ModelReplaySU2.quantileLinear(sorted.clone(), 2 / 3.0);
            for (int side = 0; side < 2; side++) {
                int cnt = 0, hit = 0;
                for (int t = 0; t < n; t++) {
                    boolean sel = side == 0 ? v[t] <= q1 : v[t] >= q2; int u = e.devStart + t;
                    if (sel && (nAct > 150 ? r.act[u] : true)) { cnt++; if (r.hit[u]) hit++; }
                }
                if (cnt < 60) continue;
                double rate = hit / (double) cnt, gap = rate - base;
                if (gap < worstGap) { worstGap = gap; worst = new String[]{kind, side == 0 ? "LOW" : "HIGH", String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", rate), String.valueOf(cnt), String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%+.4f", gap)}; }
            }
        }
        return worst;
    }
```

#### P23 父代不丢弃：名人堂·谱系·跨活动延续

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P20；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U11, U12；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P024

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- SU1 刷新时种群从固定种子重来；优秀父代下一次活动不再参与。

**做什么（目标）**

精英直接进入下一代；名人堂保留历代最好的 12 个并持续作为父代；进化状态（种群、名人堂、分层冠军、统筹统计、修复记录）压缩存库，下次活动接着跑；谱系表记录每个子代的父代与算子。

1. ModelEvolutionSU2.State + ModelStateCodecSU2（gzip，≤4 MB）+ su2_lineage。

```java
    public static byte[] encode(ModelEvolutionSU2.State s) {
        try {
            ByteArrayOutputStream bytes = new ByteArrayOutputStream();
            try (DataOutputStream out = new DataOutputStream(new GZIPOutputStream(bytes))) {
                out.writeInt(MAGIC);
                writeList(out, s.population); writeList(out, s.hallOfFame);
                out.writeInt(s.tiers.size()); for (Map.Entry<String, ModelEvolutionSU2.Ranked> e : s.tiers.entrySet()) { out.writeUTF(e.getKey()); writeRanked(out, e.getValue()); }
                out.writeBoolean(s.champion != null); if (s.champion != null) writeRanked(out, s.champion);
                writeStats(out, s.kindStats); writeStats(out, s.opStats);
                out.writeInt(s.effectiveGenerations);
                int notes = Math.min(200, s.repairNotes.size()); out.writeInt(notes);
                for (int i = s.repairNotes.size() - notes; i < s.repairNotes.size(); i++) out.writeUTF(s.repairNotes.get(i));
                out.writeInt(s.committees.size());                            // R14: committee champions per tier
                for (Map.Entry<String, CommitteeSU2> e : s.committees.entrySet()) { out.writeUTF(e.getKey()); writeLong(out, e.getValue().text()); }
                out.writeDouble(s.committeeJudge);
                out.writeInt(s.atomPrior.size());                              // R15: research memory (atom prior)
                for (Map.Entry<Integer, double[]> e : s.atomPrior.entrySet()) { out.writeInt(e.getKey()); out.writeDouble(e.getValue()[0]); out.writeDouble(e.getValue()[1]); }
                out.writeInt(s.priorSeen.size()); for (String k : s.priorSeen) writeLong(out, k);
            }
            byte[] b = bytes.toByteArray();
            if (b.length > MAX) throw new IllegalStateException("SU2_EVOLUTION_STATE_TOO_LARGE");
            return b;
        } catch (IOException impossible) { throw new IllegalStateException(impossible); }
    }
```

```java
    /** Frequency-aware stability fitness (P26): shrunk fold rates, mean - 0.25 spread, frequency floor. */
    public static double fitness(Result r, int a, int b, int folds, double minCov, double kappa) {
        double[] v = new double[folds]; int nAll = 0;
        for (int f = 0; f < folds; f++) {
            int x = a + (int) Math.floor((b - a) * (double) f / folds), y = a + (int) Math.floor((b - a) * (double) (f + 1) / folds);
            int n = r.actions(x, y), k = r.hits(x, y); nAll += n;
            v[f] = (k + 0.3 * kappa) / (n + kappa);
        }
        double mean = 0, mx = -1, mn = 2; for (double q : v) { mean += q; mx = Math.max(mx, q); mn = Math.min(mn, q); }
        mean /= folds;
        double cov = nAll / (double) (b - a), pen = cov >= minCov ? 0 : (minCov - cov) * 2;
        return mean - 0.25 * (mx - mn) - pen;
    }
```

```java
    /**
     * act/hit/missing over the evaluation calendar [a,b); incumbentAct/Hit on the same periods (may be null).
     * minActs: minimum ACT periods (e.g. 600); floor: block floor (e.g. 0.28); minCoverage: e.g. 0.15.
     */
    public static Verdict evaluate(boolean[] act, boolean[] hit, boolean[] missing, boolean[] incAct, boolean[] incHit,
                                   int a, int b, int look, double rootAlpha, int minActs, double floor, double minCoverage) {
        int n = 0, k = 0, miss = 0;
        for (int t = a; t < b; t++) { if (missing != null && missing[t]) { miss++; continue; } if (act[t]) { n++; if (hit[t]) k++; } }
        double cov = (n) / (double) Math.max(1, b - a);
        double rate = n == 0 ? 0 : k / (double) n;
        double alpha = alphaForLook(rootAlpha, look);
        // missing periods are scored as acted-and-missed: the strategy cannot hide its absences
        double lower = hoeffdingLower(k, n + miss, alpha);
        double worst = 1; int step = (b - a) / 4;
        for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
            int bn = 0, bk = 0; int x = a + i * step, y = i == 3 ? b : a + (i + 1) * step;
            for (int t = x; t < y; t++) if (act[t]) { bn++; if (hit[t]) bk++; }
            worst = Math.min(worst, bn == 0 ? 0 : bk / (double) bn);
        }
        double paired = Double.NaN;
        if (incAct != null) {
            // per-period paired difference in hits (each side counts 1 for an acted hit), bounded in [-1,1]
            int m = b - a; double s = 0;
            for (int t = a; t < b; t++) s += (act[t] && hit[t] ? 1 : 0) - (incAct[t] && incHit[t] ? 1 : 0) - 0.3 * ((act[t] ? 1 : 0) - (incAct[t] ? 1 : 0));
            double mean = s / m; paired = mean + Math.sqrt(2 * Math.log(1 / alpha) / m);   // upper bound of the paired excess (range [-1,1])
        }
        String reason = "PASS";
        if (n < minActs) reason = "ACTS_LT_" + minActs;
        else if (cov < minCoverage) reason = "COVERAGE_LT_" + minCoverage;
        else if (!(lower > 0.30)) reason = "LOWER_BOUND_NOT_ABOVE_NULL_30";
        else if (worst < floor) reason = "BLOCK_BELOW_FLOOR";
        else if (incAct != null && paired < 0) reason = "SIGNIFICANTLY_WORSE_THAN_INCUMBENT_ON_SAME_PERIODS";
        return new Verdict(reason.equals("PASS"), reason, n, k, miss, rate, lower, cov, worst, paired);
    }
```

```java
    /** Daily maintenance: evaluate the current mainline member and issue a certificate if the live evidence passes. */
    public static String maintain(DbStore store, long seq) {
        SQLiteDatabase r = store.readable();
        String member = ModelStoreSU2.currentChoice(r, seq);
        boolean incumbent = member.startsWith("XI_INCUMBENT:");                  // the old method is certified by the same rule
        if (!member.startsWith("SU2:") && !incumbent) return "SU2_CERT_NO_SU2_MAINLINE";
        String genome = member.substring(member.indexOf(':') + 1);
        if (read(r, genome, System.currentTimeMillis()) != null) return "SU2_CERT_VALID";
        long from = seq - MainlineBridgeSU2.WINDOW;
        boolean[][] me = ModelStoreSU2.record(r, genome, from, seq);
        boolean[][] inc = null;
        if (!incumbent) for (String[] m : ModelStoreSU2.league(r)) if (m[1].equals("XI_INCUMBENT")) inc = ModelStoreSU2.record(r, m[3], from, seq);
        int look; try (Cursor c = r.rawQuery("SELECT looks FROM su2_alpha_look WHERE genome_key=?", new String[]{genome})) { look = (c.moveToFirst() ? c.getInt(0) : 0) + 1; }
        SelectiveEvidenceSU2.Verdict v = SelectiveEvidenceSU2.evaluate(me[0], me[1], null, inc == null ? null : inc[0], inc == null ? null : inc[1], 0, MainlineBridgeSU2.WINDOW,
            look, MainlineBridgeSU2.ROOT_ALPHA, MainlineBridgeSU2.MIN_ACTS, MainlineBridgeSU2.BLOCK_FLOOR, MainlineBridgeSU2.MIN_COVERAGE);
        // calibration from earlier ACT rows (phat recorded at freeze time)
        double[] ph = new double[MainlineBridgeSU2.WINDOW]; boolean[] hh = new boolean[MainlineBridgeSU2.WINDOW]; int n = 0;
        try (Cursor c = r.rawQuery("SELECT phat,hit FROM su2_live_decision WHERE genome_key=? AND sequence_ordinal>=? AND sequence_ordinal<? AND act=1 AND hit IS NOT NULL AND phat IS NOT NULL ORDER BY sequence_ordinal",
            new String[]{genome, String.valueOf(from), String.valueOf(seq)})) { while (c.moveToNext() && n < ph.length) { ph[n] = c.getDouble(0); hh[n] = c.getInt(1) == 1; n++; } }
        SelectiveCalibrationSU2.Platt cal = SelectiveCalibrationSU2.fit(ph, hh, n, 10.0);
        double[] adj = new double[n]; for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) adj[i] = cal.apply(ph[i]);
        double ece = SelectiveCalibrationSU2.ece(adj, hh, n);
        final int lk = look; final boolean pass = v.pass && ece <= 0.04;
        String program; try (Cursor c = r.rawQuery("SELECT canonical_text FROM su2_model_genome WHERE genome_key=?", new String[]{genome})) { if (!c.moveToFirst()) return "SU2_CERT_GENOME_MISSING"; program = HashingSU1.text(c.getString(0)); }
        final String prog = program; final long now = System.currentTimeMillis();
        store.coordinatorForResearchR952().runAtomicWriteOrThrowF01("SU2_CERT_LOOK", db -> {
            db.execSQL("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO su2_alpha_look(genome_key,looks) VALUES(?,0)", new Object[]{genome});
            db.execSQL("UPDATE su2_alpha_look SET looks=? WHERE genome_key=? AND looks=?", new Object[]{lk, genome, lk - 1});  // every look spends alpha, pass or fail
            if (pass) {
                String id = HashingSU1.fields("SU2_CERTIFICATE_V1", genome, prog, String.valueOf(from), String.valueOf(seq), String.valueOf(lk), String.valueOf(now));
                db.execSQL("INSERT INTO su2_certificate(certificate_id,genome_key,program_hash,window_start,window_end,acts,hits,lower_bound,coverage,worst_block,paired_upper,look,alpha,calibration_a,calibration_b,ece,issued_at,expires_at) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)",
                    new Object[]{id, genome, prog, from, seq, v.acts, v.hits, v.lower, v.coverage, v.worstBlock, Double.isNaN(v.pairedUpper) ? null : v.pairedUpper, lk, SelectiveEvidenceSU2.alphaForLook(MainlineBridgeSU2.ROOT_ALPHA, lk), cal.a, cal.b, ece, now, now + 86400000L});
            }
            return 0;
        });
        return (pass ? "SU2_CERT_ISSUED" : "SU2_CERT_REFUSED:" + (v.pass ? "ECE_GT_004" : v.reason)) + "|acts=" + v.acts + "|rate=" + String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", v.rate) + "|lower=" + String.format(java.util.Locale.ROOT, "%.4f", v.lower) + "|look=" + lk;
    }
```

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/SelectiveCalibrationSU2.java` 第 18–32 行：

```java
    /** Fit on ACT rows < t only (caller guarantees causality). */
    public static Platt fit(double[] phat, boolean[] hit, int n, double ridge) {
        double a = 1, b = 0;
        for (int it = 0; it < 25; it++) {
            double ga = ridge * (a - 1), gb = ridge * b, haa = ridge, hab = 0, hbb = ridge + 1e-9;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
                double x = logit(phat[i]), p = 1 / (1 + Math.exp(-(a * x + b))), e = p - (hit[i] ? 1 : 0), w = p * (1 - p);
                ga += e * x; gb += e; haa += w * x * x; hab += w * x; hbb += w;
            }
            double det = haa * hbb - hab * hab; if (Math.abs(det) < 1e-12) break;
            double da = (hbb * ga - hab * gb) / det, db = (haa * gb - hab * ga) / det;
            a -= da; b -= db; if (Math.abs(da) + Math.abs(db) < 1e-9) break;
        }
        return new Platt(a, b);
    }
```

```java
    public static Prepared prepare(DbStore store, V51Models.PredictionContext context, V51Models.PositionAction[] actions) {
        K3ActionSpaceEngine.validateOrThrow(actions);
        long seq = TargetIssueResolver.sequenceOrdinalSU1(context.targetIssue);
        String member = ModelStoreSU2.currentChoice(store.readable(), seq);
        boolean incumbent = member.startsWith("XI_INCUMBENT:");
        if (!member.startsWith("SU2:") && !incumbent) return null;
        String genome = member.substring(member.indexOf(':') + 1);
        CertificateSU2.Authority a = CertificateSU2.read(store.readable(), genome, System.currentTimeMillis());
        if (a == null) return null;
        int position, mask; double phat; boolean act;
        try (Cursor c = store.readable().rawQuery("SELECT position,number_mask,act,phat FROM su2_live_decision WHERE genome_key=? AND sequence_ordinal=? AND hit IS NULL", new String[]{genome, String.valueOf(seq)})) {
            if (!c.moveToFirst()) return null;
            position = c.getInt(0); mask = c.getInt(1); act = c.getInt(2) == 1; phat = c.isNull(3) ? Double.NaN : c.getDouble(3);
        }
        if (!act) return null;                                             // the strategy waits for its window
        float[][] P = incumbent ? IncumbentLoopSU2.pendingTable(seq) : ModelLiveLoopSU2.pendingTable(genome, seq);
        if (P == null) return null;                                         // never re-derive after the freeze
        int[] k = new int[3]; int j = 0; for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) if ((mask & (1 << n)) != 0) k[j++] = n + 1;
        int headOrdinal = K3ActionSpaceEngine.globalOrdinal(position, k[0], k[1], k[2]);
        double[] mass = new double[1200]; int headIndex = -1;
        for (int i = 0; i < 1200; i++) {
            V51Models.PositionAction x = actions[i];
            if (K3ActionSpaceEngine.globalOrdinal(x.position, x.n1, x.n2, x.n3) == headOrdinal) headIndex = i;
            float[] p = P[x.position - 1];
            mass[i] = Math.rint(((double) p[x.n1 - 1] + p[x.n2 - 1] + p[x.n3 - 1]) * 1e9) / 1e9;
        }
        if (headIndex < 0) throw new IllegalStateException("SU2_HEAD_NOT_IN_ACTION_SPACE");
        final int hi = headIndex; Integer[] order = new Integer[1200]; for (int i = 0; i < 1200; i++) order[i] = i;
        Arrays.sort(order, (x, y) -> { if (x == hi) return y == hi ? 0 : -1; if (y == hi) return 1; int d = Double.compare(mass[y], mass[x]);
            return d != 0 ? d : Integer.compare(K3ActionSpaceEngine.globalOrdinal(actions[x].position, actions[x].n1, actions[x].n2, actions[x].n3), K3ActionSpaceEngine.globalOrdinal(actions[y].position, actions[y].n1, actions[y].n2, actions[y].n3)); });
        double p = Double.isNaN(phat) ? 0.30 : new SelectiveCalibrationSU2.Platt(a.a, a.b).apply(phat);
        String recipe = "{\"contract\":\"" + CONTRACT + "\",\"certificate\":\"" + a.certificate + "\",\"genome\":\"" + genome + "\",\"program\":\"" + a.program + "\",\"target\":\"" + context.targetIssue + "\",\"position\":" + position + ",\"mask\":" + mask + "}";
        String digest = HashingSU1.text(recipe);
        V51Models.PositionAction[] out = new V51Models.PositionAction[1200];
        for (int rank = 0; rank < 1200; rank++) {
            int i = order[rank]; V51Models.PositionAction x = actions[i];
            out[i] = new V51Models.PositionAction(x.position, x.n1, x.n2, x.n3, x.n4, mass[i], mass[i], rank == 1199 ? 0 : Math.max(0, mass[i] - mass[order[rank + 1]]), x.thirdMargin, x.entropy, x.stability, x.metaFeatures, x.fingerprint, rank + 1,
                V51ChecksumEngine.sha256("CERTIFIED_EXACT_K3_R974|" + x.checksum + '|' + digest + '|' + i + '|' + rank + '|' + mass[i]));
        }
        K3ActionSpaceEngine.validateOrThrow(out);
        return new Prepared(a, out, out[headIndex], recipe, p);
    }
```

### 批次 F · 存储·检测报告·验收出口

#### P31 全软件物理字节预算 + 目录普查（报告第一页就能看出 30 GB 在哪）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P01；补丁 07-P31；覆盖要求 U22, U40；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P030

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 5.4.3 报告只量了数据库 272,158,720 字节与 WAL（实时 753,992），完全看不到 files/ 下的报告任务、恢复包、CAS、训练输入、模型包——“两条 30 G”无从定位。

**做什么（目标）**

每次报告与研究面板都给出按类别的实际字节：DATABASE / WAL / REPORTS / REPORT_RECOVERY / RESEARCH_CAS / TRAINING_CAS / MODEL_BUNDLES / CACHE / OTHER，及最大的 8 个文件；su2_storage_budget 记录每类上限（总 1.5 GB）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. StorageCensusSU2.measure：有界遍历（20 万项、深度 12、不跟随符号链接）。
2. ReportExportServiceCP4：在文件清单之后写入 STORAGE_CENSUS.txt 成员。

```java
    public static String category(String relative) {
        String r = relative.replace('\\', '/');
        if (r.startsWith("databases/") && r.endsWith("-wal")) return "WAL";
        if (r.startsWith("databases/")) return "DATABASE";
        if (r.startsWith("files/r981_reports/recovery/") || r.startsWith("files/r981_reports/saf-parts/") || r.startsWith("files/r981_reports/failures/")) return "REPORT_RECOVERY";
        if (r.startsWith("files/r981_reports/") || r.startsWith("cache/r923_reports/")) return "REPORTS";
        if (r.startsWith("files/retention-cas-cp12/") || r.startsWith("files/retention-snapshot-r73/")) return "RESEARCH_CAS";
        if (r.startsWith("files/research-input-v2/")) return "TRAINING_CAS";
        if (r.startsWith("files/model-bundles-r929/")) return "MODEL_BUNDLES";
        if (r.startsWith("cache/") || r.startsWith("code_cache/")) return "CACHE";
        return "OTHER";
    }
```

#### P32 数据库文件真正缩小（auto_vacuum 一次性转换）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P31；补丁 08-P32；覆盖要求 U22；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P032

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- DbHelper.onConfigure（:308）每次打开都执行 PRAGMA auto_vacuum=INCREMENTAL——对已用 NONE 建库的数据库无效（必须 VACUUM 后才生效）；StorageReclaimR943（:33–36）在 mode==0 时只返回 LEGACY_COMPACTION_REQUIRED 并进入冷却，从不压缩。5000 期上限删行后，文件永远停在历史最高水位。

**做什么（目标）**

安静窗口里一次性执行 auto_vacuum=INCREMENTAL + VACUUM + WAL TRUNCATE；之后 StorageReclaimR943 的增量回收路径真正生效；超过 768 MB 的库走现有 R82 离线重建。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. DatabaseCompactionSU2.convertIfNeeded：检查事务/实时压力/空闲页比例（≥25%）/磁盘余量（≥2×库+64 MB）。
2. DbHelper：去掉无效 pragma，保留 journal_size_limit，增加 wal_autocheckpoint=1000。
3. StorageReclaimR943：mode 0 分支调用转换。

**文件：3 个（新增 1 / 修改 2 / 文档 0 / 检查 0；删除 0 / 整文件重写 0）**

| `app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/DbHelper.java` | MODIFY 锚点修改 | onConfigure | ecc021c659d31635…（8733 行；私有 9ec590ca 相同） | 补丁 08-P32 |
| `app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/StorageReclaimR943.java` | MODIFY 锚点修改 | mode 0 分支 | 4e5a657a931266da…（107 行；私有 9ec590ca 相同） | 补丁 08-P32 |

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/DatabaseCompactionSU2.java` 第 23–38 行：

```java
    /** Decide; executed by StorageReclaimR943.quiet() on the non-transactional writer handle. */
    public static String convertIfNeeded(SQLiteDatabase db, long freeDiskBytes) {
        if (db.inTransaction()) return "YIELD_TRANSACTION";
        if (RealtimePressureGateR811.hasActiveCriticalWork()) return "YIELD_REALTIME";
        long mode = scalar(db, "auto_vacuum"), pages = scalar(db, "page_count"), free = scalar(db, "freelist_count"), page = scalar(db, "page_size");
        long bytes = new File(db.getPath()).length();
        if (mode == 2) return "ALREADY_INCREMENTAL";
        boolean worth = free > 1024 && free * 4 > pages;                // >= 25% reusable
        if (!worth && mode == 0 && pages * page < (64L << 20)) return "SMALL_NO_ACTION";
        if (bytes > MAX_INLINE_BYTES) return "OFFLINE_REBUILD_REQUIRED|bytes=" + bytes;     // R82 explicit maintenance
        if (freeDiskBytes < 2 * bytes + (64L << 20)) return "DISK_TOO_FULL_FOR_VACUUM|need=" + (2 * bytes + (64L << 20));
        db.execSQL("PRAGMA auto_vacuum=INCREMENTAL");
        db.execSQL("VACUUM");                                           // rewrites the file; auto_vacuum now takes effect
        try (Cursor c = db.rawQuery("PRAGMA wal_checkpoint(TRUNCATE)", null)) { c.moveToFirst(); }
        return "CONVERTED|before=" + bytes + "|after=" + new File(db.getPath()).length() + "|mode=" + scalar(db, "auto_vacuum");
    }
```

#### P33 每期增长的表有寿命；报告大表只导出最新行

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P31；补丁 05-P07, 09-P33；覆盖要求 U16, U22, U40；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P031, P032, P033

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 5.4.3 报告：research_row_revision_v2 184,964 行、v57_runtime_event_r926 72,883 行，而开奖只有 4,995 期（3.5 天）；runtime_event 只能经存档协议删除（触发器 trg_r926_runtime_event_no_delete），归档进 CAS 只是换地方累积。
- ReportDiagnosticEvidenceR64 把约 90 张表的“全部行全部文本”写进一个 JSON（本次 121,368,680 字节），表越大报告越大。

**做什么（目标）**

SU2 自己的每期记录有上限：原始决定保留 10,080 期后折叠为日汇总；过期研究基因组清理；报告中超过 20,000 行的表只导出最新 5,000 行 + 精确总数（完整表仍可通过显式原始导出取得）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. RetentionRulesSU2.foldDecisions / pruneResearchGenomes（每次最多 2,000 行，短事务；WITHOUT ROWID 表按序号区间删除，兼容 SQLite 3.8）。
2. ReportMetadataStreamR81.writeRows 增加 fromRowid 重载；ReportDiagnosticEvidenceR64 按行数分支。
3. runtime_event 的去重/TTL 作为 R926 写入端的后续卡（P33-b），本包给出规则：同 (stage,target,detail) 合并为首末次+计数。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/RetentionRulesSU2.java` 第 27–36 行：

```java
    public static int foldDecisions(SQLiteDatabase db, long currentSequence, long now) {
        if (!db.inTransaction()) throw new IllegalStateException("SU2_RETENTION_WRITER");
        long cut = currentSequence - RAW_DECISION_PERIODS;
        db.execSQL("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO su2_live_daily(genome_key,day_key,periods,actions,hits,closed_at) "
            + "SELECT genome_key,CAST(sequence_ordinal/1440 AS INTEGER),count(*),sum(act),sum(CASE WHEN act=1 THEN hit ELSE 0 END),? FROM su2_live_decision "
            + "WHERE sequence_ordinal<? AND hit IS NOT NULL GROUP BY genome_key,CAST(sequence_ordinal/1440 AS INTEGER)", new Object[]{now, cut});
        // WITHOUT ROWID table and minSdk-21 SQLite (no row values): delete a bounded range of the oldest periods
        db.execSQL("DELETE FROM su2_live_decision WHERE hit IS NOT NULL AND sequence_ordinal<? AND sequence_ordinal<(SELECT min(sequence_ordinal)+60 FROM su2_live_decision)", new Object[]{cut});
        return changes(db);
    }
```

#### P34 检测报告体积预算：失败恢复包不再复制整库、恢复包有界、配额真正执行

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P31；补丁 10-P34；覆盖要求 U22, U40；覆盖粗糙方案卡 P032, P033, P034

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 根因 1：ReportJobJournalCP12.writeFailureArtifact（:201–247）每次完整报告失败，都把整个 snapshot.db 复制进 recovery/<请求>/<sha>.zip——与是否勾选原始证据无关。
- 根因 2：ReportPublicationCP12.pruneRequestMetadata（:121）对这些恢复目录保留最多 256 个、7 天。报告导不出来→再点导出→每次多一份整库副本：几十次就是几十 GB。
- 根因 3：ReportPublicationCP12.enforceQuota 写了但从未被调用；ReportExportServiceCP4（:255）publishedQuotaBytes=Long.MAX_VALUE；FULL_REPORT 阶段截止时间为 Long.MAX_VALUE（:318），任务可无限挂起。

**做什么（目标）**

恢复包只含异常、请求与观测（只有用户勾选原始证据且库 ≤256 MB 时才附库）；重恢复物最多保留最近 3 个、24 小时；已发布报告总额 512 MB 并在发布时强制执行；每阶段最长 30 分钟后让出并续传。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. ReportBudgetSU2：recoveryMayIncludeDatabase、pruneHeavyRecovery、PUBLISHED_QUOTA_BYTES、deadline。
2. 四处补丁：writeFailureArtifact 前置判断；pruneRequestMetadata 开头清理；reserveDisk 配额；publish 中调用 enforceQuota；ActiveControl.enter 截止时间。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/ReportBudgetSU2.java` 第 27–35 行：

```java
    /** Recovery ZIP may carry snapshot.db only if the user asked for raw evidence and it is small enough. */
    public static boolean recoveryMayIncludeDatabase(File requestJson, long snapshotBytes) {
        if (snapshotBytes <= 0 || snapshotBytes > RECOVERY_DATABASE_LIMIT || requestJson == null || !requestJson.isFile() || requestJson.length() > 65536) return false;
        try (InputStream in = new FileInputStream(requestJson)) {
            byte[] b = new byte[(int) requestJson.length()]; int off = 0, n; while (off < b.length && (n = in.read(b, off, b.length - off)) > 0) off += n;
            String s = new String(b, 0, off, StandardCharsets.UTF_8).replace(" ", "");
            return s.contains("\"includeRawEvidence\":true");
        } catch (IOException e) { return false; }
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/ReportBudgetSU2.java` 第 37–52 行：

```java
    /** Delete heavy recovery artefacts (recovery/<id>, saf-parts/<id>) beyond the newest RECOVERY_KEEP or older than 24h. Small acks stay. */
    public static int pruneHeavyRecovery(File root, long now) throws IOException {
        int removed = 0;
        for (String kind : new String[]{"recovery", "saf-parts"}) {
            File dir = new File(root, kind); File[] items = dir.listFiles(); if (items == null) continue;
            List<File> ds = new ArrayList<>(); for (File f : items) if (f.isDirectory() && f.getName().matches("[0-9a-f]{64}")) ds.add(f);
            ds.sort((a, b) -> Long.compare(b.lastModified(), a.lastModified()));
            for (int i = 0; i < ds.size(); i++) {
                File d = ds.get(i);
                if (i < RECOVERY_KEEP && now - d.lastModified() < RECOVERY_MAX_AGE_MS) continue;
                if (!d.getCanonicalFile().getParentFile().equals(dir.getCanonicalFile())) throw new IOException("REPORT_RECOVERY_PATH");
                deleteTree(d); removed++;
            }
        }
        return removed;
    }
```

`scripts/run_r13_checks.py` 第 97–130 行：

```python
def l2(rerun):
    if rerun:
        run("L2:rerun_batch", ["bash", "scripts/run_r13_batch.sh"])
    rules, ok = [], True

    def rule(name, cond, detail):
        nonlocal ok
        rules.append({"rule": name, "ok": bool(cond), "detail": detail})
        ok &= bool(cond)
        print("%-46s %s  %s" % ("L2:" + name, "PASS" if cond else "FAIL", detail), flush=True)

    sets = ["world_NULL", "world_REGIME", "world_CROSS", "world_DRIFT", "world_MIX",
            "real_repo_10k", "real_report_543", "control_repo_shuffled", "control_report_shuffled"]
    R = {s: _j(s) for s in sets}
    for s in sets:
        v = R[s]["mainline_vs_xi"]
        rule("never_worse_than_XI:" + s, v["p_mainline_worse_than_xi"] > 0.05,
             "mainline %s vs XI %s, p(worse)=%.3f, periods on XI %d/%d" % (v["mainline_hit_rate"], v["xi_hit_rate"],
                                                                          v["p_mainline_worse_than_xi"], v["periods_on_xi"], v["periods_total"]))
        m = R[s]["mainline_track"]
        rule("frequency_floor:" + s, m["coverage"] >= 0.12, "mainline coverage %.3f" % m["coverage"])
    for s in ("world_NULL", "control_repo_shuffled", "control_report_shuffled"):
        m = R[s]["mainline_track"]
        rule("no_false_edge:" + s, m["binomial_p_vs_30"] > 0.01, "mainline %.4f, p=%.4f" % (m["hit_rate"], m["binomial_p_vs_30"]))
    for s in ("world_REGIME", "world_CROSS", "world_DRIFT", "world_MIX"):
        best = max(R[s]["tier_tracks"].values(), key=lambda t: t["wilson_lower_95"])
        rule("structure_captured:" + s, best["wilson_lower_95"] > 0.30,
             "best tier %.4f@%.2f wilson %.4f" % (best["hit_rate"], best["coverage"], best["wilson_lower_95"]))
    m = R["world_REGIME"]["mainline_track"]
    rule("mainline_beats_30_on_REGIME", m["wilson_lower_95"] > 0.30, "%.4f@%.2f wilson %.4f" % (m["hit_rate"], m["coverage"], m["wilson_lower_95"]))
    REC.append({"layer": "L2", "name": "L2:rules", "ok": ok, "rules": rules})
    return ok


```

```python
def l3(repo):
    ok = True
    man = json.load(open(ROOT / "manifests" / "r13_projects.json", encoding="utf-8"))
    app = Path(repo) / "app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro"
    wt = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="r13wt")) / "wt"
    run("L3:worktree", ["git", "worktree", "add", "--detach", str(wt), man["baseline"]["public_commit"]], cwd=repo)
    bad = []
    for f, sha in man["before_sha256"].items():
        p = wt / "app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro" / f
        if not p.exists() or hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest() != sha:
            bad.append(f)
    ok &= not bad
    REC.append({"layer": "L3", "name": "L3:before_sha", "ok": not bad, "mismatch": bad})
    print("%-46s %s %s" % ("L3:before_sha (%d files)" % len(man["before_sha256"]), "PASS" if not bad else "FAIL", bad), flush=True)
    patches = sorted(str(p) for p in (ROOT / "code/patches").glob("*.patch"))
    am = run("L3:git_am_01_to_11", ["git", "-c", "user.name=r13", "-c", "user.email=r13@invalid", "am", "-q"] + patches, cwd=wt)
    ok &= am
    if am:
        # symbol-level compile of every patched SU2 class against the real SU1 sources (+ Android stubs),
        # then the SU1-program adapter test (ProgramModelSU2 needs AtomLiftSU1 / ProgramTreeSU1 ...)
        W = wt / "app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro"
        su1 = [W / (f + ".java") for f in SU1_FOR_L3]
        su2 = sorted(W.glob("strategyv2/*SU2.java")) + [W / f for f in ("RetentionRulesSU2.java", "StorageCensusSU2.java",
                                                                          "DatabaseCompactionSU2.java", "ReportBudgetSU2.java")]
        have = {p.name for p in su1 + su2}
        stubs = [p for p in (ROOT / "reference/stubs").rglob("*.java") if p.name not in have]
        cls = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp(prefix="r13l3"))
        test = ROOT / "reference/java_l3/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ProgramModelSU2Test.java"
        c = run("L3:javac_patched_SU2_(%d_files)" % len(su2), ["javac", "--release", "8", "-nowarn", "-encoding", "UTF-8", "-d", str(cls)]
                + [str(p) for p in su1 + su2 + stubs] + [str(test)])
        ok &= c
        # the 15 modified app files depend on Android / org.json: parse-only javac proves the patched text is valid Java
        mods = [str(W / f) for f in man["before_sha256"]]
        ok &= run("L3:javac_parse_15_modified_files", ["javac", "-XDshould-stop.ifError=PARSE", "-XDshould-stop.ifNoError=PARSE",
                                                       "-encoding", "UTF-8", "-d", str(cls)] + mods)
        if c:
            ok &= run("L3:ProgramModelSU2Test", ["java", "-cp", str(cls), "com.ethk3.novaonepro.strategyv2.ProgramModelSU2Test",
                                                 str(ROOT / "reference/fixtures/r13_parity/draws.txt")], expect="PROGRAM_ADAPTER_PASS")
    run("L3:worktree_remove", ["git", "worktree", "remove", "--force", str(wt)], cwd=repo)
    return ok
```

### 批次 G · R14/R14b 创造引擎深化（校准·裁判·委员会·原子网络·反馈进化）

#### P36 在线温度校准：每个成员的概率先校准，再融合、再门控（CAL）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P05, P17；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U06, U16；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 已知真相世界上逐族诊断（reference/replay/diagnose_engine.py）：六族模型的预测对数似然全部低于均匀分布——过度自信约 2.5–3 倍。漂移世界 seed 2 前推 [6000,16000)：KALMAN 比均匀差 0.427 nat/位置·期，MSDIR 差 0.004，HMM 差 0.005；校准后全部转正（+0.0004 至 +0.0019）。
- 过度自信直接伤害两件事：置信门（MASS）挑出的是“最自信”而不是“最准”的期；静态融合被最尖锐的成员支配。R13 的 gate 在已知真相世界上平均丢掉 4–7 个百分点（同一模型的选号，理想挑期 vs 自身置信挑期）。
- 真实数据上（两份）六族的最优 β 都≈0：模型在真实开奖上没有可校准的信息——校准后表接近均匀，系统据此不出手而不是硬出手（INFORMATIVE 守卫）。

**做什么（目标）**

P_cal(t)[p] = softmax(β·log P(t)[p])，β∈[0,2] 每 250 期用最近 2,500 期全部 10 个位置的对数似然以 Newton 法重估（凹函数，3 步，热启动）；1,500 期前输出均匀表；严格因果。校准后的表若最佳位置 top3 质量 ≤ 0.3（机会水平）则任何门都不出手。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. CalibrationSU2.calibrate（回放）与 CalibrationSU2.Live（每期）：同一 Newton 步、同一窗口，输出逐位一致；Live 状态 = 2,500 期原始表环 + 标签（每成员约 1 MB），写入检查点。
2. ModelGenomeSU2 增加 calibrated 标志（规范文本 SU2|CAL=1|…，旧文本仍可解析）；STATIC 融合在校准程序里用归一化权重（几何平均，不再相加放大）。
3. ModelReplaySU2：Library.getCal(member)、evaluateTable（MASS 门 + INFORMATIVE 守卫）；ModelLiveSU2.Hub 为每个成员维护校准器，predict 时出校准表、settle 时喂标签。
4. Python 参考 foundry14.calibrate / Workbench14：所有 R14 模拟都在校准表上进行。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/CalibrationSU2.java` 第 25–44 行：

```java
    /** Newton update of beta on rows L[i][p][n] with realised numbers y[i][p] (0-based); returns the new beta. */
    static double fitBeta(double[][][] L, int[][] y, int n, double beta) {
        for (int it = 0; it < ITERS; it++) {
            double gs = 0, hs = 0; long cnt = 0;
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                double[] l = L[i][p]; double mx = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
                for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) mx = Math.max(mx, beta * l[k]);
                double z = 0; double[] e = new double[10];
                for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) { e[k] = Math.exp(beta * l[k] - mx); z += e[k]; }
                double m1 = 0, m2 = 0; for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) { double pr = e[k] / z; m1 += pr * l[k]; m2 += pr * l[k] * l[k]; }
                gs += l[y[i][p]] - m1; hs += m2 - m1 * m1; cnt++;
            }
            double g = gs / cnt, h = -hs / cnt;
            if (h > -1e-12) break;
            double nb = Math.min(2.0, Math.max(0.0, beta - g / h));
            if (Math.abs(nb - beta) < 1e-6) { beta = nb; break; }
            beta = nb;
        }
        return beta;
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/CalibrationSU2.java` 第 60–73 行：

```java
    /** Batch (replay) form over a whole table. */
    public static float[][][] calibrate(float[][][] P, int[][] draws) {
        int T = P.length; float[][][] out = new float[T][][];
        for (int t = 0; t < Math.min(START, T); t++) out[t] = uniform();
        double beta = 1.0;
        for (int t0 = START; t0 < T; t0 += REFIT) {
            int lo = Math.max(0, t0 - WINDOW), n = t0 - lo;
            double[][][] L = new double[n][10][]; int[][] y = new int[n][10];
            for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) { L[i][p] = logRow(P[lo + i][p]); y[i][p] = draws[lo + i][p] - 1; }
            beta = fitBeta(L, y, n, beta);
            for (int t = t0; t < Math.min(t0 + REFIT, T); t++) out[t] = apply(beta, P[t]);
        }
        return out;
    }
```

#### P37 委员会冠军：每个频率层的冠军是裁判选出的前 5 个不同概率表的几何平均融合

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P36, P38；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U11, U25, U29, U33；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R13 每个频率层只取一个“最好”的程序，挑中幸运者的概率高（赢家诅咒，见 P38）。select_study.py：在同一批创造出的程序上，前 5 名几何平均委员会在 15% 频率比被选中的单个程序高 2–4 个百分点（规则世界 37.3–37.7% vs 33.3%，交叉 33.2–33.3%，漂移 33.3–33.4%，混合 33.0–33.4%）。
- 委员会必须和单个程序一样可冻结、可结算、可检查点、可进联赛、可上主链，否则只是离线数字。

**做什么（目标）**

CommitteeSU2：P = normalise(exp(mean_k log P_k))，K ≤ 5、成员概率表互不相同（同表不同门不重复计数）；最佳位置 top3、MASS 门按层频率 15%/30%/100%。规范文本 SU2C|COV=0.15|G=<成员文本>##… 是身份；与单程序走同一条实时车道（LiveStrategySU2）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. CommitteeSU2.fuse 与 Python 逐位一致（numpy 同序两两求和）；evaluate = 融合表 + evaluateTable。
2. LiveStrategySU2 接口：ModelLiveSU2（单程序）与 CommitteeLiveSU2（委员会）都实现 decide/settle/pendingTable/write/readInto；ModelLiveLoopSU2 的运行时集合改为 LinkedHashMap<String, LiveStrategySU2>，按文本前缀创建。
3. CommitteeLiveSU2：成员子策略共享 Hub（同一模型只算一次），720 期置信环做块因果门；n<100 不出手。
4. 存储：schema canonical_text CHECK 接受 'SU2|' 与 'SU2C' 前缀；ModelStoreSU2.saveCommittee（origin_op=COMMITTEE，state=LEAGUE）；ModelStateCodecSU2 魔数 0x53553214（旧 0x53553213 仍可读）保存委员会；ModelFoundryJobSU2 第 8 代把三层委员会写入联赛（“·委员会”标签）。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/CommitteeSU2.java` 第 60–71 行：

```java
    /** Geometric-mean fusion of tables (numpy-identical reductions). */
    public static float[][] fuse(float[][][] tables) {
        int K = tables.length; float[][] out = new float[10][10]; double[] L = new double[10], E = new double[10];
        for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
            double mx = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
            for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double s = 0; for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) s += Math.log(Math.min(1.0, Math.max(1e-9, (double) tables[k][p][n]))); L[n] = s / K; mx = Math.max(mx, L[n]); }
            for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) E[n] = Math.exp(L[n] - mx);
            double z = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(E, 0, 10);
            for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) out[p][n] = (float) (E[n] / z);
        }
        return out;
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/CommitteeLiveSU2.java` 第 27–50 行：

```java
    public ModelLiveSU2.Decision decide() {
        float[][][] tabs = new float[subs.length][][];
        for (int k = 0; k < subs.length; k++) { subs[k].decide(); tabs[k] = subs[k].lastPooled; }
        float[][] P = CommitteeSU2.fuse(tabs);
        lastPooled = P; lastPooledT = t;
        double[] mass = new double[10]; int[][] top = new int[10][3]; Integer[] ord = new Integer[10];
        for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
            final float[] row = P[p]; for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) ord[i] = i;
            Arrays.sort(ord, (a, b) -> Double.compare((double) row[b] - b * 1e-12, (double) row[a] - a * 1e-12));
            double s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { top[p][i] = ord[i]; s += (double) row[ord[i]]; }
            mass[p] = Math.rint(s * 1e9) / 1e9;
        }
        int p1 = 0; for (int p = 1; p < 10; p++) if (mass[p] > mass[p1]) p1 = p;
        curP1 = p1; curK3 = top[p1].clone(); curGs = mass[p1];
        boolean act = false;
        if (t >= startMeta) {
            if (c.coverage >= .999) act = curGs > ModelReplaySU2.INFORMATIVE;
            else {
                if ((t - startMeta) % 50 == 0) blockThreshold();
                act = thrValid && curGs >= thr && curGs > ModelReplaySU2.INFORMATIVE;
            }
        }
        return new ModelLiveSU2.Decision(t, p1 + 1, (1 << curK3[0]) | (1 << curK3[1]) | (1 << curK3[2]), act, curGs, curGs);
    }
```

- CommitteeLiveSU2Test：R14_LIVE_EQUALS_REPLAY_PASS（t=5000 检查点 7.26 MB，晚出生委员会自举一致）
- test_su2_sql.py：SU2C 行写入成功，其他前缀被 CHECK 拒绝（OR IGNORE 静默丢弃 → 断言不存在）

**回滚**：R14 关闭时不生成委员会；已存委员会行 state 改 RETIRED 即退出实时集。

#### P38 共识裁判：用无噪声的“期望命中”而不是幸运的实现命中来挑程序

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P26；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U16, U26；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- select_study.py（已知真相世界，4 世界 × 多种子）：程序过去的实现胜率与它未来的真实质量秩相关只有 0.03–0.60，而程序自身质量在过去/未来之间很稳定（无噪声质量相关 0.65–0.96）——R13 的选择主要在挑运气（赢家诅咒），这是 R13 丢边际的最大来源。
- 用模型共识 Q 下“这些出手的期望命中”评分，秩相关升到 0.63–0.95；但它只看模型自己相信的东西——这一缺陷在 R14 验证中暴露（P40）。

**做什么（目标）**

Q = 中心模型 + 名人堂的校准表按最近 2,500 期全位置对数似然做贝叶斯平均（每代重建）；程序得分 = 其出手期在 Q 下的期望命中（折内收缩 κ=200、折间极差惩罚、覆盖不足惩罚）。R14b 起改为经验贝叶斯混合（P40）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. ModelEvolutionSU2.buildConsensus / judge / cum（每程序每期全位置对数似然的前缀和）/ tableKey（门与频率不改变表）。
2. dev/val 适应度在 r14 下都用裁判；名人堂、层冠军、委员会成员都按裁判排序；有效代 = F15 委员会裁判值创新高。
3. Python：foundry14.consensus / judge_fitness / central_programs。

```java
    /** Bayesian model average of the programs' tables, weights from the trailing-window log-likelihood. */
    void buildConsensus(List<ModelGenomeSU2> progs) {
        int T = lib.draws().length, M = progs.size(); float[][][][] tabs = new float[M][][][]; double[][] cs = new double[M][];
        for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) { tabs[m] = table(progs.get(m)); cs[m] = cum(progs.get(m)); }
        float[][][] q = new float[T][10][10];
        for (int t = 0; t < Math.min(CalibrationSU2.START, T); t++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) Arrays.fill(q[t][p], 0.1f);
        for (int t0 = CalibrationSU2.START; t0 < T; t0 += CalibrationSU2.REFIT) {
            int lo = Math.max(0, t0 - CalibrationSU2.WINDOW); double[] s = new double[M]; double mx = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
            for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) { s[m] = cs[m][t0] - cs[m][lo]; mx = Math.max(mx, s[m]); }
            double[] w = new double[M]; for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) w[m] = Math.exp(s[m] - mx); double z = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(w, 0, M); for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) w[m] /= z;
            for (int t = t0; t < Math.min(t0 + CalibrationSU2.REFIT, T); t++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) {
                double v = 0; for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) v += w[m] * tabs[m][t][p][n]; q[t][p][n] = (float) v; }
        }
        Q = q;
    }
```

- ModelEvolutionR14Test：规则世界前推 F15 36.86%（n=860，Wilson 34.20%）、F30 36.59%、F100 34.65%；NULL 世界无假边际（R14_JAVA_FOUNDRY_PASS）

**回滚**：r14=false 即回到 R13 适应度。

#### P39 原子网络族 ATOMNET：原子世界成为可学习的底层基（不再是一次一个固定温度的原子）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P10, P36；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U06, U09, U10, U25, U27, U29, U32；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R13 的原子成员 P = softmax(τ·原子)：符号、温度固定，一次一个；168 个提升原子彼此孤立，不能学到“遗漏只在某些位置有用”“短窗频率与长窗频率要互相权衡”；而且 Java 进化从不生成原子成员（只有 Python 参考有 ATOM 种类）——原子世界在手机上并未真正支撑策略。
- 留出结构世界（worlds_x.py：R13 六族不是为它们设计的）静态检查：HOTCOLD（动量/回补两种状态）上最好的 R13 族 MSDIR 15% 频率 38.1%，ATOMNET 41.3%（理论上限 45.7%）；PARITY（奇偶/大小组偏）HMM 35.6%，ATOMNET 36.1%；PERIODIC（每 8 期相位）与 LAG2MAP（滞后 2 任意映射）上所有 R13 族都≈30%（抓不到）。
- 注册表的 PERIODICITY 原子只说“号码是否以周期 m 重复”，不说“当前相位该出哪个号”，所以周期结构无法被预测利用。

**做什么（目标）**

ATOMNET 在线多项对数线性模型：z[p,n] = b + Σ_f A[p,n,f]·(u[f]+v[p,f]) + Σ_r [n==map_r(D[t−l,p+d])]·w[p,r]；A = 选中的注册表原子（10 族 118 个，AtomEvaluatorCP4 语义）+ 新增 PHASE_FREQUENCY 原子（周期 2–24 × 窗 20/60，共 20 个）+ 原子×原子交叉项（原子级的“交叉交集”）+ 奇偶/大小组原子（3 尺度 + 上期）；r = 跨位置关系原子（滞后 × 源偏移 × SAME/PLUS1/MINUS1/MIRROR）。u 全位置共享、v/w 分位置；RMS 归一化在线步长 + L2 + 累加器衰减（可跟随状态切换）。学到的权重就是每个原子的“贡献”，反馈给子代（P41）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. AtomStreamSU2：每期从 1,600 期环（整数计数，结果与 Python 累加和形式逐位相同）计算 138 个原子；缺失值按行均值填补、按位置 z 标准化（numpy 同序两两求和）、截断 ±4；检查点保存环与最近两次出现时刻。目录顺序由 scripts/gen_atom_catalog_su2.py 从 Python 目录生成。
2. AtomNetSU2：OnlineModelsSU2.Model 实现；参数文本 atoms=1-3-6…, pairs=none|ixj, lags=none|1-2, group=0|1, lr, l2, gd；credit() 输出每原子 |u|+mean|v|、组原子均值、关系最大 |w|。
3. ModelReplaySU2.build 增加 ATOMNET；ModelGenomeSU2.R14B_KINDS 接受 ATOMNET；实时 Hub/检查点/校准与其他族相同。
4. Python 参考 atomnet.py（规约顺序固定，Java 可逐位复现）；foundry14：Workbench14 承载 ATOMNET 并保存学习贡献；中心模型加入默认 ATOMNET（12 个多尺度原子），让共识 Q 认识原子世界里的结构。

```java
    double[][] raw(Spec s) {
        double[][] v = new double[10][10];
        switch (s.family) {
            case "FREQUENCY": return freq(s.i("window"));
            case "ROLLING_VARIANCE_INDICATOR": {
                double[][] f = freq(s.i("window"));
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) v[p][n] = f[p][n] * (1 - f[p][n]);
                return v;
            }
            case "FREQUENCY_SLOPE": {
                int w = s.i("window");
                if (t < w) { for (double[] r : v) Arrays.fill(r, Double.NaN); return v; }
                long lo = t - w; long[][] bins = new long[10][10], wt = new long[10][10];
                for (long u = lo; u < t; u++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) { int a = x(u, p); bins[p][a]++; wt[p][a] += u - lo; }
                double den = w * (w * (double) w - 1.0) / 12;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) v[p][n] = ((double) wt[p][n] - (w - 1) * (double) bins[p][n] / 2) / den;
                return v;
            }
            case "OMISSION":
            case "OMISSION_RATE": {
                boolean rate = s.family.equals("OMISSION_RATE"); int w = rate ? s.i("window") : 0;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) {
                    double g = last[p][n] >= 0 ? (double) (t - last[p][n]) : Double.NaN;
                    double o = g - 1;
                    v[p][n] = !rate ? o : (t < w ? Double.NaN : (Double.isNaN(o) ? Double.NaN : Math.min(o, w) / w));
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "HOT_COLD_Z": {
                int r = s.i("recent"), R = s.i("reference");
                double[][] a = freq(r), z = freq(R);
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) {
                    double var = z[p][n] * (1 - z[p][n]) * (1.0 / r - 1.0 / R);
                    v[p][n] = var > 1e-12 ? (a[p][n] - z[p][n]) / Math.sqrt(Math.max(var, 1e-12)) : Double.NaN;
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "MARKOV_SCORE": {
                int w = s.i("window"); double alpha = s.d("alpha");
                if (t < w + 1) { for (double[] r : v) Arrays.fill(r, Double.NaN); return v; }
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    int from = x(t - 1, p); long[] row = new long[10];
                    for (long j = Math.max(0, t - w - 1); j <= t - 2; j++) if (x(j, p) == from) row[x(j + 1, p)]++;
                    long sum = 0; for (long c : row) sum += c;
                    for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) v[p][n] = (row[n] + alpha) / (sum + 10 * alpha);
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "LAG_DIGIT_EQ": {
                int l = s.i("lag");
                if (t >= l) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) v[p][x(t - l, p)] = 1.0;
                return v;
            }
            case "GAP_LAST_GAP": {
                int w = s.i("window");
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++)
                    v[p][n] = t >= w && prev[p][n] >= t - w ? (double) (last[p][n] - prev[p][n]) : Double.NaN;
                return v;
            }
            case "PERIODICITY": {
                int w = s.i("window"), L = s.i("period");
                if (t < w + L) { for (double[] r : v) Arrays.fill(r, Double.NaN); return v; }
                long lo = t - w; double nn = w;
                long[][] A = new long[10][10], B = new long[10][10], AB = new long[10][10];
                for (long u = lo; u < t; u++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) { int cur = x(u, p), lg = x(u - L, p); A[p][lg]++; B[p][cur]++; if (cur == lg) AB[p][cur]++; }
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) {
                    double a = A[p][n], b = B[p][n], ab = AB[p][n];
                    double den = Math.sqrt(Math.max((a - a * a / nn) * (b - b * b / nn), 0));
                    v[p][n] = den > 1e-12 ? (ab - a * b / nn) / Math.max(den, 1e-12) : Double.NaN;
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "GAP_MEAN_GAP": case "GAP_VAR_GAP": case "GAP_GAP_Z": return gapStats(s.family, s.i("window"));
            case "XI_SUPPORT": {
                float[][][] Z = experts(); int k = s.i("k");
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    int[][] rank = new int[8][];
                    for (int e = 0; e < 8; e++) rank[e] = ranks(Z[e][p]);
                    for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double[] in = new double[8]; for (int e = 0; e < 8; e++) in[e] = rank[e][n] < k ? 1.0 : 0.0; v[p][n] = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(in, 0, 8) / 8; }
                }
                return v;
            }
            default: throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_ATOM_FAMILY " + s.family);
        }
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/AtomNetSU2.java` 第 144–172 行：

```java
    public void update(int[] draw) {
        OnlineModelsSU2.requireDraw(draw);
        features();
        double[] g = new double[100];
        for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) g[p * 10 + n] = pr[p * 10 + n] - (n == draw[p] - 1 ? 1.0 : 0.0);
        for (int i = 0; i < 100; i++) { double gb = g[i] + l2 * b[i]; Gb[i] = gd * Gb[i] + gb * gb; b[i] -= lr * gb / Math.sqrt(Gb[i]); }
        if (Fd > 0) {
            double[] gv = new double[10 * Fd];
            for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int f = 0; f < Fd; f++) {
                double s = g[p * 10] * X[(p * 10) * Fd + f];
                for (int n = 1; n < 10; n++) s = s + g[p * 10 + n] * X[(p * 10 + n) * Fd + f];
                gv[p * Fd + f] = s;
            }
            double[] gu = new double[Fd];
            for (int f = 0; f < Fd; f++) { double s = gv[f]; for (int p = 1; p < 10; p++) s = s + gv[p * Fd + f]; gu[f] = s + l2 * u[f]; }
            double l2v = 10 * l2;
            for (int i = 0; i < 10 * Fd; i++) gv[i] = gv[i] + l2v * v[i];
            for (int f = 0; f < Fd; f++) { Gu[f] = gd * Gu[f] + gu[f] * gu[f]; u[f] -= lr * gu[f] / Math.sqrt(Gu[f]); }
            for (int i = 0; i < 10 * Fd; i++) { Gv[i] = gd * Gv[i] + gv[i] * gv[i]; v[i] -= lr * gv[i] / Math.sqrt(Gv[i]); }
        }
        if (Fr > 0 && anyRel()) {
            for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int r = 0; r < Fr; r++) {
                int k = p * Fr + r, i = ridx[k]; boolean ok = i >= 0;
                double gw = (ok ? g[p * 10 + i] : 0.0) + l2 * w[k];
                if (ok) { Gw[k] = gd * Gw[k] + gw * gw; w[k] -= lr * gw / Math.sqrt(Gw[k]); }
            }
        }
        stream.update(draw);
    }
```

#### P40 经验贝叶斯裁判 + 名人堂家族上限：堵住“共识自我强化”的漏洞

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P38；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U16, U26, U33；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R14 验证中发现（validation/r14，漂移世界 seed 2）：R13 部署了 HMM + 适用状态门（15% 频率前推期望命中 33.8%），R14 却部署了清一色 MSDIR 的委员会（32.5%），而校准后的 HMM 表本身更好（同门 34.3% vs 32.4%）——R14 在这个世界上比 R13 差，违反“不得劣于旧版”。
- 根因：裁判按共识 Q 下的期望命中打分，Q 又由名人堂喂入；名人堂被裁判选进来的 MSDIR 近亲占满后，Q 成了“MSDIR 的意见”，与它一致的程序得分更高——自我强化回路（日志 consensus_top_weight 第 1 代 0.899 后由 MSDIR 家族合计占据）。
- judge_study.py（6 个世界/种子/检查点）：前 5 委员会前推期望命中，纯 Q 裁判 vs 经验贝叶斯：规则 s2 44.0→46.7、规则 s3 38.1→43.7、交叉 s2 32.6→32.9、交叉 s3 35.1→35.0、漂移 s2 34.0→34.0、漂移 s3 33.9→34.8；平均 +1.5 个百分点，没有一例差 0.1 以上。留一族共识（LOFO）在交叉世界失败（秩相关 −0.14），已否决。

**做什么（目标）**

J = (实现命中 + κ·出手期在 Q 下的平均期望命中)/(出手数 + κ) − 覆盖惩罚，κ=300：实现部分无偏但有噪声，Q 部分精确但有偏，二者按样本量加权；共识 Q 中名人堂每个家族签名最多 2 个程序。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. foundry14.judge_eb / diverse；ModelEvolutionSU2.judgeEb，r14b 时 judge() 走经验贝叶斯，buildConsensus 前对名人堂按家族签名截断。
2. κ 的选择来自已知真相世界（100/300/1000 三档结果几乎相同，取中间值），不在真实数据上调。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` 第 137–144 行：

```java
    /** R14b judge (P40): realised hits shrunk toward the consensus expectation of the same decisions,
     *  J = (hits + kappa * mean_acted Q-expected) / (acts + kappa) - coverage penalty (foundry14.judge_eb). */
    double judgeEb(ModelReplaySU2.Result r, int a, int b) {
        int n = 0, k = 0; double s = 0;
        for (int t = a; t < b; t++) if (r.act[t]) { n++; if (r.hit[t]) k++; int p = r.position[t] - 1; double e = 0; for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++) if ((r.mask[t] & (1 << j)) != 0) e += Q[t][p][j]; s += e; }
        double q = n > 0 ? s / n : 0.3, cov = n / (double) (b - a), pen = cov >= 0.12 ? 0 : (0.12 - cov) * 2;
        return (k + EB_KAPPA * q) / (n + EB_KAPPA) - pen;
    }
```

#### P41 反馈驱动的进化：原子贡献引导子代 + 开放关系搜索（一代比一代强的机制）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P21, P22, P39；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U09, U11, U12, U25, U26, U29, U32, U33, U34, U39；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R13/R14 的逐代曲线几乎是平的（validation/r14 漂移世界：8 代内 F15 委员会期望命中 32.2–32.8%）：成员参数只在 3–4 档网格上，第 0–1 代就把可达空间搜完了，后续代没有更好的东西可找。
- LAG2MAP 留出世界（滞后 2、任意号码映射）：LOGLIN 只有 5 种固定源组合，RELBAYES 只有 4 种映射，结构在搜索空间之外，任何代数都找不到。

**做什么（目标）**

搜索空间变成组合式的，而且子代沿着父代的分析结果走：ATOMNET 子代读取父代在检查点前学到的原子贡献——删去贡献最低的原子、探索贡献最高原子的同族邻域、把两个最强原子做交叉项、按贡献开关组原子/关系原子；LOGLIN 子代可增删移动任意 (源偏移, 滞后) 对。新算子 credit：给没有原子网络的父代嫁接默认原子网络。所有改动记入修复日志（父代分、子代分、原因）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. foundry14：_credit_child / _loglin_child / op_apply14 / Director14 / screen_seeds14（加入 4 个 ATOMNET 种子）/ register_r14b（只在 R14b 引擎注册，R13/R14 搜索空间逐位不变）。
2. ModelEvolutionSU2（r14b）：OPS_R14B 增加 credit；randomMember/jitter 处理 ATOMNET 与开放 LOGLIN；credit(member) 只用 valEnd 之前的开奖重放计算（不泄漏未来）；种子与 Director 的种类集合包含 ATOMNET。
3. 贡献注记格式：ATOM_<原子>_LOWEST_CREDIT=…|CHILD_DROPS_IT、…_HIGHEST_CREDIT|CHILD_EXPLORES_<邻域原子>、TWO_STRONGEST_ATOMS_CROSSED|… 等，写入 repairNotes 并进入报告。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` 第 335–389 行：

```java
    /** Children of an ATOMNET member follow the parent's learned credit (atomnet credit-guided edits, foundry14._credit_child). */
    ModelGenomeSU2.Member creditChild(ModelGenomeSU2.Member m) {
        Map<String, String> p = m.params;
        List<Integer> atoms = new ArrayList<>(); for (int x : AtomNetSU2.ints(p.get("atoms"))) atoms.add(x);
        List<int[]> pairs = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList(AtomNetSU2.pairList(p.get("pairs"))));
        List<Integer> lags = new ArrayList<>(); for (int x : AtomNetSU2.ints(p.get("lags"))) lags.add(x);
        boolean group = "1".equals(p.get("group")); double lr = Double.parseDouble(p.get("lr")), l2 = Double.parseDouble(p.get("l2")), gd = Double.parseDouble(p.get("gd"));
        List<int[]> mols = new ArrayList<>(Arrays.asList(AtomNetSU2.molList(p.get("mols"))));
        Map<String, Double> cr = credit(m); lastNote = "";
        double r0 = rng.nextDouble();
        if (r15 && cr != null && r0 < 0.25) {
            if (!mols.isEmpty() && rng.nextDouble() < 0.4) {
                int[] d = mols.get(0); double dv = Double.POSITIVE_INFINITY;
                for (int[] x : mols) { double v = cr.getOrDefault("M" + x[0] + "@" + x[1], 0.0); if (v < dv) { dv = v; d = x; } }
                mols.remove(d); lastNote = "MOLECULE_" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[d[0]].name() + "|" + AtomStreamSU2.STATES[d[1]].name() + "_LOWEST_CREDIT|CHILD_DROPS_IT";
            } else if (!atoms.isEmpty() && mols.size() < 4) {
                int top = atoms.get(0); double tv = -1; for (int a : atoms) { double v = cr.getOrDefault("A" + a, 0.0); if (v > tv) { tv = v; top = a; } }
                int j = rng.nextInt(AtomStreamSU2.states()); mols.add(new int[]{top, j});
                lastNote = "ATOM_" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[top].name() + "_HIGHEST_CREDIT|CHILD_MAKES_IT_STATE_DEPENDENT_ON_" + AtomStreamSU2.STATES[j].name();
            }
            return atomNet(atoms, pairs, lags, group, lr, l2, gd, mols);
        }
        if (cr != null && r0 < 0.75) {
            List<Integer> ranked = new ArrayList<>(atoms); ranked.sort(Comparator.comparingDouble((Integer i) -> cr.getOrDefault("A" + i, 0.0)).thenComparing(i -> i));
            double[] vals = atoms.stream().mapToDouble(i -> cr.getOrDefault("A" + i, 0.0)).sorted().toArray();
            double med = vals.length == 0 ? 0 : (vals.length % 2 == 1 ? vals[vals.length / 2] : (vals[vals.length / 2 - 1] + vals[vals.length / 2]) / 2);
            double q = rng.nextDouble();
            if (q < 0.3 && atoms.size() > 3) {
                int d = ranked.get(0); atoms.remove((Integer) d); pairs.removeIf(x -> x[0] == d || x[1] == d);
                lastNote = "ATOM_" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[d].name() + "_LOWEST_CREDIT=" + String.format(Locale.ROOT, "%.3f", cr.getOrDefault("A" + d, 0.0)) + "|CHILD_DROPS_IT";
            } else if (q < 0.6 && !ranked.isEmpty()) {
                int top = ranked.get(ranked.size() - 1); List<Integer> nb = new ArrayList<>(); for (int k : neighbours(top)) if (!atoms.contains(k)) nb.add(k);
                if (!nb.isEmpty()) { int k = nb.get(rng.nextInt(nb.size())); atoms.add(k); lastNote = "ATOM_" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[top].name() + "_HIGHEST_CREDIT|CHILD_EXPLORES_" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[k].name(); }
            } else if (q < 0.8 && ranked.size() >= 2 && pairs.size() < 3) {
                int i = ranked.get(ranked.size() - 1), j = ranked.get(ranked.size() - 2);
                boolean has = false; for (int[] x : pairs) has |= Math.min(x[0], x[1]) == Math.min(i, j) && Math.max(x[0], x[1]) == Math.max(i, j);
                if (!has) { pairs.add(new int[]{Math.min(i, j), Math.max(i, j)}); lastNote = "TWO_STRONGEST_ATOMS_CROSSED|" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[i].name() + "x" + AtomStreamSU2.CATALOG[j].name(); }
            } else {
                Double gc = cr.get("GROUP"), rc = cr.get("REL");
                if (gc != null && gc < 0.5 * med) { group = false; lastNote = "GROUP_ATOMS_LOW_CREDIT|CHILD_DROPS_THEM"; }
                else if (rc != null && rc < med) { lags.clear(); lastNote = "RELATION_ATOMS_LOW_CREDIT|CHILD_DROPS_THEM"; }
                else if (!group) { group = true; lastNote = "CHILD_TRIES_GROUP_ATOMS"; }
                else { int l = rng.nextBoolean() ? 1 : 2; if (!lags.contains(l)) lags.add(l); lastNote = "CHILD_TRIES_RELATION_ATOMS"; }
            }
        } else {
            double q = rng.nextDouble();
            if (q < 0.3) atoms.add(r15 ? priorPick(atoms) : rng.nextInt(universe()));
            else if (q < 0.5 && atoms.size() > 2) { int d = atoms.get(rng.nextInt(atoms.size())); atoms.remove((Integer) d); pairs.removeIf(x -> x[0] == d || x[1] == d); }
            else if (q < 0.7) lr = new double[]{0.02, 0.05, 0.1}[rng.nextInt(3)];
            else if (q < 0.85) gd = new double[]{1.0, 0.999, 0.995}[rng.nextInt(3)];
            else l2 = new double[]{1e-4, 1e-3}[rng.nextInt(2)];
        }
        if (atoms.isEmpty()) atoms.add(rng.nextInt(universe()));
        return atomNet(atoms, pairs, lags, group, lr, l2, gd, mols);
    }
```

```java
    /** Open relation search (P41): add, drop or move one (source offset, lag) pair of a LOGLIN member. */
    ModelGenomeSU2.Member loglinChild(ModelGenomeSU2.Member m) {
        int[][] src = ModelReplaySU2.pairs(m.params.get("sources")); List<int[]> s = new ArrayList<>(); for (int[] x : src) s.add(x.clone());
        double q = rng.nextDouble();
        if (q < 0.35 && s.size() < 5) { int[] n = {rng.nextInt(10), new int[]{1, 1, 2, 3}[rng.nextInt(4)]}; if (indexOf(s, n) < 0) s.add(n); }
        else if (q < 0.6 && s.size() > 1) s.remove(rng.nextInt(s.size()));
        else { int i = rng.nextInt(s.size()); int[] o = s.get(i); int[] n = rng.nextDouble() < 0.6 ? new int[]{(o[0] + (rng.nextBoolean() ? 1 : 9)) % 10, o[1]} : new int[]{o[0], Math.min(3, Math.max(1, o[1] + (rng.nextBoolean() ? 1 : -1)))}; if (indexOf(s, n) < 0) s.set(i, n); }
        Map<String, String> p = new TreeMap<>(m.params); p.put("sources", sourcesText(s));
        return new ModelGenomeSU2.Member(m.kind, p, m.weight);
    }
```

#### P42 可证伪的引擎诊断与留出结构世界（回答“到底有没有能做到不错胜率的公式”）

- 优先级 **P1**；依赖 P35；补丁 —（文档/检查）；覆盖要求 U16, U19, U20, U25, U28, U34, U35, U36, U38；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- “已经知道确实有算法公式可以做到不错的胜率和稳定性”这句话只有在能被证伪的设定下才有意义：已知真相世界里我们知道理论上限，能分清是创造不出、选不出还是门控丢掉；真实数据上只能看冻结前推。
- 只用 R13 自己设计的世界验证，会把“为这些世界设计的模型”误当成“通用的创造能力”。

**做什么（目标）**

三类证据并列：①已知真相世界（R13 五个 + 留出四个）上的理论上限、各族上限、选择损失、门控损失；②同数据同种子 R13 / R14 / R14b 滚动部署前推（实现命中 + 无噪声期望命中）；③真实数据与时间打乱对照上的同一流程。任何结论都同时给出对照组。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. diagnose_engine.py：ORACLE / PICK_IDEAL / PICK_ALL / GATE_MASS / INFO 分解；select_study.py：生成 vs 选择；judge_study.py：裁判偏差；worlds_x.py：HOTCOLD / PARITY / PERIODIC / LAG2MAP。
2. rolling14.py：--engine 13|14、--atomnet、--judge；输出 tier_tracks（实现）、tier_expected（无噪声）、generation_curves、IPM 主链 vs XI。
3. 结果与限制写入 docs/17，L2 规则读取 validation/r14 与 validation/r14b。

```python
def run(D_all, checkpoints, L, engine, gens=8, pop=24, seed=7, val_len=1500, Pt=None, log=None):
    T = len(D_all)
    if engine in (14, 15, 16):
        wb = G.Workbench14(D_all, None); ev = G.evaluate; camp = G.campaign
    else:
        wb = F.Workbench(D_all, None); ev = F.evaluate; camp = F.campaign
    ti = np.arange(T)
    rng = random.Random(seed)
    state = None
    bank0 = hre.ExpertBank(D_all.astype(np.int8))
    xr = hre.evaluate(bank0, D_all.astype(np.int8), hre.seed_cross_intersection(), 1500, T)
    records = {"XI": (xr.act, xr.hit & xr.act, 1500)}
    names = {"XI": "XI_INCUMBENT(用户交叉交集原方法)"}
    tier_fw, tier_eh, xi_fw, xi_eh, base_fw, rows, curves, notes = {}, {}, [], [], [], [], [], []

    def eh_of(r, a, b):
        if Pt is None:
            return None
        e = Pt[ti, r.position - 1][ti[:, None], r.numbers - 1].sum(axis=1)
        act = r.act[a:b]
        return float(e[a:b][act].mean()) if act.any() else None

    t0 = time.perf_counter()
    for c in checkpoints:
        E = {"warmup": (0, 1500), "dev": (1500, c - val_len), "val": (c - val_len, c)}
        a, b = c, min(c + L, T)
        if engine in (14, 15, 16):
            curve_eval = (lambda g, a=a, b=b: (lambda r: {"expected": eh_of(r, a, b), "realised": F.summary(r, a, b)["hit_rate"],
                                                           "coverage": F.summary(r, a, b)["coverage"]})(ev(wb, g)))
            rep = camp(wb, E, generations=gens, population=pop, rng=rng, state=state, log=log, tag=str(c), curve_eval=curve_eval)
            strategies = rep["committees"]
            curves.append({"checkpoint": c, "curve": rep["curves"]})
        else:
            rep = camp(wb, E, generations=gens, population=pop, rng=rng, state=state, log=log, tag=str(c))
            strategies = rep["state"]["tiers"]
        state = rep["state"]
        notes += rep["repair_notes"][:10]
        row = {"checkpoint": c, "deploy": [a, b], "tiers": {}}
        if engine in (14, 15, 16):
            row["director"] = rep.get("director")
            row["evaluated_programs"] = rep.get("evaluated")
            pr = (state or {}).get("prior") or {}
            if pr:
                import atomnet as AN
                row["research_memory_top_atoms"] = [(AN.atom_name(i), round(v[1], 3), v[0]) for i, v in
                                                    sorted(pr.items(), key=lambda kv: -kv[1][1])[:10]]
        if engine >= 15 and strategies.get("F15") is not None:
            try:
                import matrices as MX
                if wb._asrc is None:
                    import atomnet as AN
                    wb._asrc = AN.AtomSource(wb.D, wb.bank)
                row["matrices"] = MX.snapshot(wb, strategies["F15"], ev(wb, strategies["F15"]), c, L)
            except Exception as e:                       # reporting must never break a validation run
                row["matrices"] = {"error": repr(e)[:300]}
        for t in TIERS:
            g = strategies.get(t)
            if g is None:
                continue
            r = ev(wb, g)
            s = F.summary(r, a, b)
            s["expected"] = eh_of(r, a, b)
            tier_fw.setdefault(t, []).append(s)
            if s["expected"] is not None:
                tier_eh.setdefault(t, []).append((s["actions"], s["expected"]))
            row["tiers"][t] = {"formula": g.formula()[:400], "forward": s}
            k = g.key()
            if k not in records:
                records[k] = (r.act, r.hit & r.act, c)
                names[k] = "%s@%d" % (t, c)
        xs = F.summary(R13._Fixed(xr.act, xr.hit & xr.act, "XI"), a, b)
        xi_fw.append(xs)
        if Pt is not None:
            e = Pt[ti, xr.position - 1][ti[:, None], xr.numbers - 1].sum(axis=1) if hasattr(xr, "position") else None
            if e is not None and xr.act[a:b].any():
                xi_eh.append((int(xr.act[a:b].sum()), float(e[a:b][xr.act[a:b]].mean())))
        base = ev(wb, F.Genome((("EXPERT", F._freeze({"w": (0, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0), "tau": 1.0}), 1.0),), gate="MASS", coverage=1.0))
        base_fw.append(F.summary(base, a, b))
        if Pt is not None:
            row["oracle_forward"] = worlds.oracle_frontier(Pt[a:b], D_all[a:b])
        rows.append(row)
        if engine in (14, 15, 16):                      # deployed genomes for post-hoc layers (P49 stage layer)
            DEPLOYED.append({"checkpoint": c, "deploy": [a, b], "tiers": dict(strategies),
                             "hof": [h for h in (state or {}).get("hof", [])[:8]]})
        if log:
            log({"checkpoint_done": c, "tiers": {t: (v["forward"]["hit_rate"], v["forward"]["coverage"], v["forward"].get("expected"))
                                                 for t, v in row["tiers"].items()}, "elapsed_s": round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1)})
    members = [IPM.Member(k, names[k], k == "XI", a_, h_, b_) for k, (a_, h_, b_) in records.items()]
    blocks, swlog, vs = IPM.tracks(members, checkpoints, L, T)

    def wmean(lst):
        n = sum(x for x, _ in lst)
        return round(sum(x * y for x, y in lst) / n, 4) if n else None
    return {"engine": engine, "mainline_track": R13._merge(blocks), "mainline_switch_log": swlog, "mainline_vs_xi": vs,
            "tier_tracks": {t: R13._merge(v) for t, v in tier_fw.items()},
            "tier_expected": {t: wmean(v) for t, v in tier_eh.items()},
            "xi_track": R13._merge(xi_fw), "xi_expected": wmean(xi_eh) if xi_eh else None,
            "always_act_base_track": R13._merge(base_fw), "checkpoints": rows, "generation_curves": curves,
            "repair_notes": notes[:40], "seconds": round(time.perf_counter() - t0, 1)}


```

### 批次 H · R15 原子世界·分子·矩阵·研究记忆·原子蒸馏

#### P43 原子世界补全 + 学习型分子：注册表 28 族全部可用，分子由学习而不是固定模板产生

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P39；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U29, U30；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 源码与 5.4.3 报告核查：research_atom_spec_v2 25,350 行中 25,220 行停在 DISCOVERED；research_atom_admission_cp12 0 行。准入规则是“单个原子、样本外 ≥240 期、均值−4 倍标准误 > 0”（r952/AtomAdmissionCP12.java:8 POLICY_HASH、:24），弱信号的单个原子几乎不可能通过——而弱信号恰恰要联合起来才有用。
- 25,350 个原子是 28 个公式族在“位置×号码×窗口”上的重复：独立可计算张量只有 168 个评分 + 150 个条件序列（R12 已提升 13 族）。GAP 均值/方差/Z、熵、条件熵、数字均值/方差、差分、方向、JS/PSI 漂移、区制分数 12 族从未能在策略里计算。
- 分子：assets/research/cp4_molecule_seeds.jsonl 4,200 个种子只有 4 种模板（阈值、两原子加权和、两种条件），常数固定为 0/0.1/0.3，角色全部 FEATURE_ONLY；导入 4,995 期只完成 893 个（research_molecule_import_cp12 = IMPORTING），用分子建家族的工厂在 4,200 个全部导入前一律等待（r952/ModernFamilyFactoryCP12.java:28、:173 guardComplete → WAIT_MOLECULE_CATALOG_VERIFICATION），research_molecule_use_cp12 与 research_family_creation_cp12 都是 0 行。
- 实测（已知真相世界，family 静态检查）：把 GAP、XI、16 个分子一次堆进同一个原子网络，15% 层前推期望命中：动量/回补世界 41.3%→38.5%（只加 16 个分子 38.7%）、规则世界 37.9%→37.0%、漂移世界 33.8%→32.2%、奇偶世界 34.8%→32.2%——噪声输入稀释信号。原子必须由按贡献筛选的进化来选，不能全部堆入。

**做什么（目标）**

注册表全部有用的族都能被策略计算：评分原子 165 个（R12 的 118 + 相位 20 + GAP×3 族×5 窗 + 用户 XI 方法拆出的 8 个专家 + 4 个 Top-k 支持度），状态原子 112 个（9 族全部注册表参数）；分子 = 原子 × 状态（状态高/低时分别学权重），由进化按贡献生成与删除。准入不再逐个原子过门，而是整条策略在样本外过证书（P27）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. atoms_r15.py：GAP 统计按 AtomWindowCP4.Window.gap 语义；状态序列按 AtomEvaluatorCP4 语义（缺失=NaN），状态指示 = 高于自身前 300 个有效值的中位数为 +1，否则 −1，不足 100 个为 0；不提升的族及原因：TRANSITION_P1（以末位号码为 from 即 MARKOV_SCORE α=0，已提升）、MUTUAL_INFORMATION（无符号，只说滞后有信息不说哪个号）、4 个策略记录模板（由 IPM 降级检验与适用状态门承担）。
2. fixtures/atom_catalog_r15.json 由 scripts/build_atom_catalog_r15.py 从真实注册表（sha256 记录在文件内）生成；AtomStreamSU2 的原子与状态目录由 scripts/gen_atom_catalog_su2.py 生成，与 Python 逐项同序。
3. atomnet.mols：分子输入 = 原子 i × 状态 j 指示；贡献引导子代可“让最强原子随某状态变化”或删去贡献最低的分子。
4. 旧分子导入与旧矩阵引擎保持原样（删除 0，回滚安全），SU2 不依赖它们；它们占用的 IMPORT 时间片是否关闭，留到 L4 设备验收时按 CPU 预算决定。
