`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/AtomStreamSU2.java` 第 542–599 行：

```java
    /** State series value at target u (draws < u), all positions; NaN = Missing. */
    double[] stateAt(Spec s, long u) {
        double[] v = new double[10]; Arrays.fill(v, Double.NaN);
        switch (s.family) {
            case "DELTA_NUMERIC": case "DIRECTION": {
                int l = s.i("lag"); if (u < l + 1) return v;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) { double d = x(u - 1, p) - x(u - 1 - l, p); v[p] = s.family.equals("DIRECTION") ? Math.signum(d) : d; }
                return v;
            }
            case "ENTROPY": case "ROLLING_MEAN_DIGIT": case "ROLLING_VARIANCE_DIGIT": {
                int w = s.i("window"); if (u < w) return v;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    double[] c = countsAt(u, w, p);
                    if (s.family.equals("ENTROPY")) { v[p] = entropy(c); continue; }
                    double m = 0; for (int d = 0; d < 10; d++) m += d * c[d]; m /= w;
                    if (s.family.equals("ROLLING_MEAN_DIGIT")) { v[p] = m; continue; }
                    double q = 0; for (int d = 0; d < 10; d++) q += (d - m) * (d - m) * c[d]; v[p] = q / w;
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "CONDITIONAL_ENTROPY": {
                int w = s.i("window"), l = s.i("lag"); if (u < w + l) return v;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    double[][] J = new double[10][10]; for (long q = u - w; q < u; q++) J[x(q - l, p)][x(q, p)] += 1;
                    double h = 0;
                    for (int a = 0; a < 10; a++) { double n = 0; for (int b = 0; b < 10; b++) n += J[a][b]; for (int b = 0; b < 10; b++) { double c = J[a][b]; if (c > 0) h -= c / w * Math.log(c / n); } }
                    v[p] = h;
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "DRIFT_JS": case "DRIFT_PSI": {
                int r = s.i("recent"), R = s.i("reference"); if (u < r + R) return v;
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    double[] rc = countsAt(u, r, p), oc = countsAt(u, r + R, p); for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) oc[k] -= rc[k];
                    double[] a = probs(rc), b = probs(oc); v[p] = s.family.equals("DRIFT_JS") ? js(a, b) : psi(a, b);
                }
                return v;
            }
            case "REGIME_SCORE": {
                int w = s.i("window"); if (u < 2L * w) return v;
                int ri = Arrays.asList(REGIMES).indexOf(s.str.get("regime"));
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    double[] c = countsAt(u, w, p), old = countsAt(u, 2 * w, p); for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) old[k] -= c[k];
                    double h = entropy(c) / Math.log(10), j = js(probs(c), probs(old));
                    double[][] J = new double[10][10]; for (long q = u - (w - 1); q < u; q++) J[x(q - 1, p)][x(q, p)] += 1;
                    double a = 0, b = 0, aa = 0, bb = 0, ab = 0;
                    for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) for (int k = 0; k < 10; k++) { double n = J[i][k]; a += n * i; b += n * k; aa += n * i * i; bb += n * k * k; ab += n * i * k; }
                    double cnt = w - 1, den = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0.0, (aa - a * a / cnt) * (bb - b * b / cnt)));
                    double corr = den <= 1e-12 ? 0.0 : (ab - a * b / cnt) / den;
                    double[] lg = {2 * (1 - h), 2 * h, 2 * Math.max(0.0, corr), 2 * Math.max(0.0, -corr), 4 * j};
                    double[] e = new double[5]; double sm = 0; for (int i = 0; i < 5; i++) { e[i] = Math.exp(lg[i]); sm += e[i]; }
                    v[p] = e[ri] / sm;
                }
                return v;
            }
            default: throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_STATE_FAMILY " + s.family);
        }
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/AtomStreamSU2.java` 第 607–631 行：

```java
    /** State indicator at the current target: +1 above / -1 not above the median of the previous 300 present values, 0 otherwise. */
    public float[] state(int j) {
        Long nt = stateNowT.get(j); if (nt != null && nt == t) return stateNow.get(j);
        Spec s = STATES[j];
        double[][] ring = stateRing.computeIfAbsent(j, k -> new double[MEDIAN_PAST][]);
        long[] when = stateTime.computeIfAbsent(j, k -> { long[] a = new long[MEDIAN_PAST]; Arrays.fill(a, -1); return a; });
        long lo = Math.max(0, t - MEDIAN_PAST);
        for (long u = lo; u < t; u++) { int k = (int) (u % MEDIAN_PAST); if (when[k] != u) { ring[k] = stateAt(s, u); when[k] = u; } }
        double[] cur = stateAt(s, t);
        float[] out = new float[10];
        for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
            if (Double.isNaN(cur[p]) || Double.isInfinite(cur[p])) continue;
            double[] col = new double[(int) (t - lo)]; int n = 0;
            for (long u = lo; u < t; u++) { double y = ring[(int) (u % MEDIAN_PAST)][p]; if (!Double.isNaN(y) && !Double.isInfinite(y)) col[n++] = y; }
            if (n < MEDIAN_MIN) continue;
            double[] c2 = Arrays.copyOf(col, n); Arrays.sort(c2);
            double med = n % 2 == 1 ? c2[n / 2] : (c2[n / 2 - 1] + c2[n / 2]) / 2;
            out[p] = cur[p] > med ? 1f : -1f;
        }
        int k = (int) (t % MEDIAN_PAST);
        // the slot of t-300 is no longer needed after this target
        ring[k] = cur; when[k] = t;
        stateNow.put(j, out); stateNowT.put(j, t);
        return out;
    }
```

#### P44 研究记忆：系统越用越会挑方向（原子先验）

- 优先级 **P1**；依赖 P41；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U34, U39；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R14b 的新原子网络从目录里均匀随机抽原子：已经在几十个程序里证明有用或无用的原子，下一次创造时仍和其他原子同等对待——系统不会“积累经验”。

**做什么（目标）**

每个被评估的原子网络在检查点报告自己学到的原子贡献（只用检查点前的开奖）；研究记忆保存每个原子的“被用次数、归一化贡献的滑动均值”，跨检查点、跨活动延续，并写入进化状态；新程序与探索性变异按“记忆贡献 + 0.02 探索”比例抽原子。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. foundry14._prior_pick / campaign 更新 prior（每个程序每个检查点只记一次）；ModelEvolutionSU2.updatePrior / priorPick；ModelStateCodecSU2 魔数 0x53553215（旧 0x53553214/13 仍可读）保存 atomPrior。
2. rolling14 输出每个检查点的记忆前 10 原子（docs/17 §7）。

```java
    /** Research memory: every atom network credited at this checkpoint reports what it learned (once per program). */
    void updatePrior(State s) {
        for (Map.Entry<String, Map<String, Double>> e : new ArrayList<>(creditCache.entrySet())) {
            if (!s.priorSeen.add(e.getKey())) continue;
            if (s.priorSeen.size() > 20000) { Iterator<String> it = s.priorSeen.iterator(); it.next(); it.remove(); }
            double tot = 0; int n = 0; for (Map.Entry<String, Double> c : e.getValue().entrySet()) if (c.getKey().startsWith("A")) { tot += c.getValue(); n++; }
            if (n == 0 || tot <= 0) continue;
            for (Map.Entry<String, Double> c : e.getValue().entrySet()) {
                if (!c.getKey().startsWith("A")) continue;
                int i = Integer.parseInt(c.getKey().substring(1)); double[] v = s.atomPrior.computeIfAbsent(i, k -> new double[2]);
                v[0] += 1; v[1] += (c.getValue() / tot * n - v[1]) / v[0];
            }
        }
    }
```

#### P45 教师矩阵：按可观测状态评估每个成员（教师）的可靠度，并直接用于融合

- 优先级 **P1**；依赖 P17, P43；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U31；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- TeacherMatrixEngine / TeacherNumberMatrixEngine（合计约 9,800 行）只给 V20 旧链候选贴“教师命中/置信/五级可靠度”字段，最终由 DecisionGate 决定；5.4.3 报告中 teacher_number_matrix、teacher_matrix_* 表全部 0 行（旧链已退役，research_mainline_contribution_r922 显示 LEGACY_MAINLINE_RETIRED）。
- 实测（已知真相世界，7 个中心模型池，4 个状态原子分别试）：与不分状态的按位置贝叶斯平均相比，15% 层前推期望命中在规则世界 0 至 +0.5 点、漂移 −0.3 至 +0.3、混合 −0.1 至 +0.1、动量/回补 −0.2 至 +0.2——有时有用、幅度小，所以只作为可进化的融合选项，由裁判决定是否采用、按哪个状态。

**做什么（目标）**

融合方式 TEACHER:<j>：每 250 期用最近 2,500 期、按状态原子 j 在每个位置的高/低分别累计各成员的对数似然，softmax 得到“教师矩阵” W[成员, 位置, 状态]；当期按状态取对应的一行融合。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. teacher.py / TeacherPoolSU2（回放 + 实时，规约顺序固定）；ModelGenomeSU2 接受 POOL=TEACHER:<j>；ModelReplaySU2.pooledTable 与 ModelLiveSU2 接入；进化的 pool 算子可选择教师矩阵与状态。

```java
    /** Replay form (Python teacher.teacher_pool). */
    public static float[][][] pool(float[][][][] Ps, int[][] draws, float[][] s) {
        int M = Ps.length, T = draws.length; float[][][] out = new float[T][][];
        double[][][] W = new double[3][10][M]; for (double[][] a : W) for (double[] b : a) Arrays.fill(b, 1.0 / M);
        float[][][] at = new float[M][][];
        for (int t0 = 0; t0 < T; t0 += REFIT) {
            if (t0 >= START) {
                int lo = Math.max(0, t0 - WINDOW), n = t0 - lo;
                double[][] L = new double[n][M * 10]; float[][] S = new float[n][];
                for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) { int u = lo + i; S[i] = s[u]; for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) L[i][m * 10 + p] = ll(Ps[m][u], p, draws[u][p] - 1); }
                weights(L, S, n, M, W);
            }
            for (int t = t0; t < Math.min(t0 + REFIT, T); t++) { for (int m = 0; m < M; m++) at[m] = Ps[m][t]; out[t] = combine(at, s[t], W); }
        }
        return out;
    }
```

#### P46 因子矩阵 / 原因矩阵 / 教师矩阵快照：每条上线策略都能用底层因子解释，原因在样本外分级

- 优先级 **P1**；依赖 P39, P45；补丁 —（文档/检查）；覆盖要求 U31, U32；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- CausalFactorMatrixEngine（11 类“因”）、PositionCauseMatrixEngine、ReasonCausalMatrixEngine（A–E 原因分级）只对旧链候选做手工打分与 Wilson 分级，每个原因单独评，且不改变概率；5.4.3 报告中 causal_factor_matrix、position_cause_matrix 表 0 行。
- 用户要求：好策略必须有可解释的底层逻辑和底层原子/因子/分子基础，并能据此分析优缺点、从底层进化。

**做什么（目标）**

每个检查点为上线的 F15 委员会冻结：因子矩阵（其中每个原子网络在每个位置对每个因子学到的有符号权重）；原因矩阵（每次出手各因子族对所选 3 码的支持度，在随后样本外区间分级 A_VALID / B_USEFUL / C_EXPLAIN_ONLY / E_FALSE）；教师矩阵（每个成员在 5 个状态原子高/低时的对数似然增益）；委员会整体的原子解释（P47）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. reference/replay/matrices.py；rolling14 --engine 15 每个检查点写入 matrices；报告与研究面板展示同一结构（施工时由 ResearchDashboard 读取联赛行的快照 JSON）。

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`reference/replay/matrices.py` 第 40–114 行：

```python
def snapshot(wb, committee, result, c, L):
    """Matrices of a deployed committee at checkpoint c, graded on [c, c+L)."""
    out = {"checkpoint": c, "factor_matrix": [], "reason_grades": {}, "teacher_matrix": {}}
    a, b = c, min(c + L, wb.T)
    act = np.nonzero(result.act[a:b])[0] + a
    sup = collections.defaultdict(list)
    seen = set()
    for sub in committee.subs:
        for kind, fp, _ in sub.members:
            if kind != "ATOMNET" or (kind, fp) not in wb.credit or (kind, fp) in seen:
                continue
            seen.add((kind, fp))
            cr = wb.credit[(kind, fp)]
            W = AN.factor_matrix_at(cr, c)
            if W is None:
                continue
            names = AN.feature_names(cr)
            # strongest factors (by mean |weight| over positions)
            imp = np.abs(W).mean(axis=0)
            for f in np.argsort(-imp)[:6]:
                out["factor_matrix"].append({"factor": names[f], "mean_abs_weight": round(float(imp[f]), 4),
                                             "by_position": [round(float(x), 3) for x in W[:, f]]})
            # per-decision support of each factor family, from the same inputs the network saw
            src = wb._asrc
            kw = G.atomnet_kwargs(dict(fp))
            feats = [src.get(i) for i in kw["atoms_idx"]] + [src.pair(i, j) for i, j in kw["pairs"]] + [src.mol(i, j) for i, j in kw["mols"]]
            if kw["group"]:
                gf = AN.group_features(wb.D)
                feats += [gf[..., k] for k in range(gf.shape[3])]
            for f, X in enumerate(feats[:len(names)]):
                fam = _family(names[f])
                for t in act:
                    p1 = result.position[t] - 1
                    k3 = result.numbers[t] - 1
                    sup[fam].append((t, float(X[t, p1, k3].mean()) * float(W[p1, f])))
    hits = {t: bool(result.hit[t]) for t in act}
    for fam, rows in sup.items():
        per_t = collections.defaultdict(float)
        for t, v in rows:
            per_t[t] += v
        pos = [t for t, v in per_t.items() if v > 0]
        neg = [t for t, v in per_t.items() if v <= 0]
        kp, kn = sum(hits[t] for t in pos), sum(hits[t] for t in neg)
        rp = kp / len(pos) if pos else None
        rn = kn / len(neg) if neg else None
        lift = (rp - rn) if (rp is not None and rn is not None) else None
        wl = _wilson(kp, len(pos))
        grade = ("A_VALID" if (lift is not None and lift > 0 and wl > 0.30 and len(pos) >= 100) else
                 "B_USEFUL" if (lift is not None and lift > 0.01) else
                 "E_FALSE" if (lift is not None and lift < -0.02) else "C_EXPLAIN_ONLY")
        out["reason_grades"][fam] = {"supported": len(pos), "hit_when_supported": rp, "hit_otherwise": rn,
                                     "lift": lift, "wilson_supported": round(wl, 4), "grade": grade}
    # atomic explanation of the deployed committee itself (P47): what its fused table relies on, in atoms
    try:
        import distill as DS
        top, r2 = DS.atomic_genome(G.table(wb, committee), wb._asrc, DS.candidates(), 1500, c, k=8)
        out["atomic_explanation"] = {"r2": round(r2, 3), "atoms": [(n, round(w_, 4)) for _, w_, n in top]}
    except Exception as e:                     # never break reporting
        out["atomic_explanation"] = {"error": repr(e)[:200]}
    # teacher matrix on the dev window
    src = wb._asrc
    states = [j for j in range(AN.n_states()) if A15.state_name(j) in TEACHER_STATES]
    y = (wb.D - 1)[:, :, None]
    for k, sub in enumerate(committee.subs):
        P = G.table(wb, sub).astype(np.float64)
        ll = np.log(np.clip(np.take_along_axis(P, y, axis=2)[:, :, 0], 1e-9, 1)) - math.log(0.1)    # [T,10]
        row = {}
        for j in states:
            s = src.state(j)[1500:c]
            l = ll[1500:c]
            hi, lo = l[s > 0], l[s < 0]
            row[A15.state_name(j)] = {"high": round(float(hi.mean()) * 1000, 3) if hi.size else None,
                                      "low": round(float(lo.mean()) * 1000, 3) if lo.size else None}
        out["teacher_matrix"]["member%d" % (k + 1)] = {"formula": sub.formula()[:160], "gain_mnat_by_state": row}
    return out
```

#### P47 原子蒸馏：任何策略（任何家族）都被拆到底层原子并从底层重建、继续进化

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P39, P43, P44；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U29, U30, U32, U33, U39；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 六族数学模型、XI、融合与委员会不是由原子直接组成的：以前只能整体比较，无法说清“它实际上依赖哪些原子、哪里强哪里弱”，也无法从底层改进。
- 实测（动量/回补世界，15% 层前推期望命中）：对中心模型做原子蒸馏并按其最强原子重建子代——BOCPD 37.7%→38.9%、HMM 34.5%→38.4%、KALMAN 36.0%→39.0%、XI 基线 32.9%→38.8%；MSDIR 38.1%→36.8%、原子网络本身 41.3%→36.9% 变差。所以蒸馏是一个“有时更好”的创造路径，由进化与裁判决定去留，而不是替换父代。

**做什么（目标）**

distill：在检查点前的开奖上，用岭回归把父代校准表的中心化对数概率拟合到中心化原子输入上，得到 R²（父代有多大比例能被原子解释）和每个原子的有符号权重（父代的原子基因）；取最强 8 个原子重建为原子网络子代，在线学习自己的权重。上线委员会的原子解释写入矩阵快照。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. distill.py（候选 = 默认原子 + R15 原子 + 研究记忆前 12 + 10 个随机）；foundry14.distill_child 与 OPS15 的 distill 算子；ModelEvolutionSU2.distill / distillChild 与 OPS_R15。

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ModelEvolutionSU2.java` 第 278–314 行：

```java
    /** Ridge fit of the parent's centred log-table on centred atom inputs over [1500, end) (every 3rd period). */
    public static Distilled distill(float[][][] P, int[][] draws, List<Integer> cand, int end, int k) {
        int F = cand.size(); double[][] XtX = new double[F][F]; double[] Xty = new double[F], sx = new double[F], sxx = new double[F]; double ss = 0; long n = 0, nx = 0;
        AtomStreamSU2 st = new AtomStreamSU2(); double[][] x = new double[F][10];
        for (int t = 0; t < end; t++) {
            if (t >= 1500 && (t - 1500) % DISTILL_STRIDE == 0) {
                float[][][] A = new float[F][][]; for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) A[f] = st.atom(cand.get(f));
                for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    double[] y = new double[10]; double my = 0; for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) { y[q] = Math.log(Math.min(1.0, Math.max(1e-9, (double) P[t][p][q]))); my += y[q]; } my /= 10;
                    for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) { double m = 0; for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) { m += A[f][p][q]; sx[f] += A[f][p][q]; sxx[f] += (double) A[f][p][q] * A[f][p][q]; } m /= 10; for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) x[f][q] = A[f][p][q] - m; }
                    nx += 10;
                    for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) {
                        double yc = y[q] - my; ss += yc * yc; n++;
                        for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) { Xty[f] += x[f][q] * yc; for (int g = f; g < F; g++) XtX[f][g] += x[f][q] * x[g][q]; }
                    }
                }
            }
            st.update(draws[t]);
        }
        if (n == 0) return null;
        double[][] Aug = new double[F][F + 1];
        for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) { for (int g = 0; g < F; g++) Aug[f][g] = (g >= f ? XtX[f][g] : XtX[g][f]) / n + (f == g ? DISTILL_RIDGE : 0); Aug[f][F] = Xty[f] / n; }
        for (int c = 0; c < F; c++) {                                           // Gaussian elimination, partial pivoting
            int piv = c; for (int r = c + 1; r < F; r++) if (Math.abs(Aug[r][c]) > Math.abs(Aug[piv][c])) piv = r;
            double[] tmp = Aug[c]; Aug[c] = Aug[piv]; Aug[piv] = tmp;
            for (int r = 0; r < F; r++) if (r != c) { double fct = Aug[r][c] / Aug[c][c]; for (int q = c; q <= F; q++) Aug[r][q] -= fct * Aug[c][q]; }
        }
        double[] w = new double[F]; for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) w[f] = Aug[f][F] / Aug[f][f];
        double res = ss; for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) { res -= 2 * w[f] * Xty[f]; for (int g = 0; g < F; g++) res += w[f] * w[g] * (g >= f ? XtX[f][g] : XtX[g][f]); }
        double r2 = ss > 0 ? 1 - res / ss : 0;
        Integer[] ord = new Integer[F]; double[] score = new double[F];
        for (int f = 0; f < F; f++) { ord[f] = f; double mu = sx[f] / nx, sd = Math.sqrt(Math.max(0, sxx[f] / nx - mu * mu)); score[f] = Math.abs(w[f]) * sd; }
        Arrays.sort(ord, (a, b) -> Double.compare(score[b], score[a]));
        int kk = Math.min(k, F); int[] at = new int[kk]; double[] ww = new double[kk];
        for (int i = 0; i < kk; i++) { at[i] = cand.get(ord[i]); ww[i] = w[ord[i]]; }
        return new Distilled(at, ww, r2);
    }
```

### 批次 I · R16 结果分析→反馈→升级：区制记忆（模式与关系在区制消失时不被遗忘）；P49 阶段与窗口层（阶段识别·延续/方向矩阵·观察出手·择期）

#### P48 区制记忆族 REGMEM / RELMEM：系统记住出现过的结构，结构重现时立即认出（由 R15 验证结果分析得出）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P13, P15, P41, P42；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U11, U34, U36, U37；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- R15 验证（区制世界，留出种子 2）逐检查点分析：第 12000–14000 期理论上限 46.6%（15% 层），冻结的 R15 委员会只有 30.0%（R13 35.1%）；第 8000 段上限 48.4%，R15 38.1%、R13 38.5%。新旧两版在同一处丢分：一个以前出现过的强结构区制重新出现，没有被认出。
- 根因（regmem_study.py 定量确认）：委员会依赖的在线 HMM 所有状态都是可学习的，后验必须落在某个状态上，长时间无结构时概率最大的那个状态吸收噪声，学到的模式被冲淡成均匀分布；校准后表退化为机会水平——R15 委员会里的 HMM 在第 10000 与 12000 段 15% 层恰好 30.0%。模式重现时只能从头再学。
- 同类问题也出现在跨位置关系上：关系在“开/关”区制间切换（CROSS 世界开启比例 48%），遗忘型关系可靠度（RELBAYES）关掉期间把关系忘掉——最好的配置只抓到可得边际的 62%。

**做什么（目标）**

两个新数学族（不是调参）：① REGMEM：K+1 状态前向滤波，状态 0 是锚定的“无结构”状态（均匀发射、永不更新），状态 1..K 是学到的区制模式，模式只按自身后验更新——区制不在时模式被保存，重现时前几期就被认出；② RELMEM：同样的锚定无结构状态 + K 个“关系区制”，发射是条件分布 z_k[p,n] = b_k[p,n] + Σ_{l,q,f} w_k[p,l,q,f]·[n = f(D[t−l,q])]（SAME/PLUS1/MINUS1/MIRROR）+ Σ_s V_k[s,p,D[t−lag_s,p+off_s],n]（任意映射，来源由开放关系搜索进化），权重按自身后验加权的对数似然梯度在线学习。两族都进入创造空间、共识 Q、种子、失败诊断（定向修复可加区制记忆成员）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. Python 参考 reference/replay/regmem.py（regmem / relmem / GRID / RELGRID）；foundry14.register_r16 只在 R16 引擎注册（R13–R15 的搜索空间逐位不变）；_relmem_child 对 RELMEM 的来源做增/删/移（与 LOGLIN 开放关系搜索同规则）；rolling14 --engine 16。
2. Java：OnlineModelsSU2.RegMem / RelMem（规约顺序与 numpy 一致，pairwise 求和）、ModelReplaySU2.build、ModelGenomeSU2.R16_KINDS、ModelEvolutionSU2.r16（种类、中心程序、种子、失败诊断、relmemChild）；ModelStateCodecSU2 魔数 0x53553216（R13–R15 状态仍可读；旧版应用读到 R16 状态会从头开始而不是崩溃）。
3. 静态实测（regmem_study.py，留出种子 2，每个模型单独、校准、自身置信门 15%，前推 [6000,16000)）：区制世界 HMM 39.1% → REGMEM 43.5%（K=4；逐段 48.4/45.5/36.6/38.7/43.6，上限 48.4/48.4/38.0/46.6/48.4）；混合世界 34.4% → 37.9%；奇偶 35.6% → 37.3%；周期 30.7% → 31.4%；跨位置关系世界 RELBAYES 最好 34.6% → RELMEM 36.2%（上限 37.4%）；漂移、动量/回补世界 HMM 更好（REGMEM −1 至 −3 点）——所以它们是新增成员族，由裁判按数据选择，不替换 HMM；无结构世界与两份真实开奖：校准后对数似然增益 ≈0、15% 门 30.0%（不报假）。
4. 工程（验证中发现）：两个 Python 参考进程同时运行时被内存 cgroup（6.27 GB）杀死 → SU2_LOWMEM 缓存上限（结果逐位相同，只是多重算）；手机端由 ModelReplaySU2.Library 的字节上限控制，不受影响。

```java
    // ----------------------------------------------------------------- R16 regime memory (P48)
    /**
     * REGMEM: hidden regimes with an ANCHORED null state (uniform, never updated) and K learned regime patterns; a
     * pattern moves only with its own posterior, so it survives while its regime is absent and is recognised as soon
     * as it returns (regmem.regmem in the Python reference). State: (null posterior, entropy over the active states,
     * strongest active posterior).
     */
    public static final class RegMem implements Model {
        final int K, S1; final double stay, lr, temper; final double[] E, A, a, pi, ll;
        public RegMem(int K, double stay, double lr, long seed, double temper, double init) {
            if (K < 1 || K > 6) throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_REGMEM_K");
            this.K = K; S1 = K + 1; this.stay = stay; this.lr = lr; this.temper = temper;
            double[] z = DeterministicRandomSU2.normals(seed, K * 100);
            E = new double[S1 * 100];
            for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                double sum = 0;
                for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double v = 0.1; if (k > 0) v *= Math.exp(init * z[(k - 1) * 100 + p * 10 + n]); E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] = v; }
                sum = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(E, k * 100 + p * 10, 10);
                for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] /= sum;
            }
            A = new double[S1 * S1];
            for (int i = 0; i < S1; i++) for (int j = 0; j < S1; j++) A[i * S1 + j] = i == j ? stay : (1 - stay) / K;
            a = new double[S1]; Arrays.fill(a, 1.0 / S1); pi = new double[S1]; ll = new double[S1];
        }
        public int stateWidth() { return 3; }
        private void prior() { for (int j = 0; j < S1; j++) { double s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < S1; i++) s += a[i] * A[i * S1 + j]; pi[j] = s; } }
        public void predict(float[][] out, float[] st) {
            prior();
            for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double v = 0; for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) v += pi[k] * E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n]; out[p][n] = (float) v; }
            regimeState(pi, K, st);
        }
        public void update(int[] d) {
            requireDraw(d);
            prior();
            double lmax = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY;
            double[] lg = new double[10];
            for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) { for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) lg[p] = Math.log(E[k * 100 + p * 10 + d[p] - 1]); ll[k] = temper * ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(lg, 0, 10); lmax = Math.max(lmax, ll[k]); }
            double z = 0; for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) { a[k] = pi[k] * Math.exp(ll[k] - lmax); z += a[k]; }
            for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) a[k] /= z;
            for (int k = 1; k < S1; k++) { double g = lr * a[k]; for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) { for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] *= (1 - g); E[k * 100 + p * 10 + d[p] - 1] += g; } }
        }
        public void write(DataOutput o) throws IOException { writeArr(o, E); writeArr(o, a); }
        public void read(DataInput in) throws IOException { readArr(in, E); readArr(in, a); }
    }
```

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/OnlineModelsSU2.java` 第 257–346 行：

```java
    /**
     * RELMEM: relation-regime memory - anchored null state + K active states whose emission is CONDITIONAL on earlier
     * draws: z_k[p,n] = b_k[p,n] + sum_{l,q,f} w_k[p,l,q,f] [n == f(D[t-l,q])] (f = SAME/PLUS1/MINUS1/MIRROR)
     * + sum_s V_k[s,p,D[t-lag_s,p+off_s],n] (arbitrary learned maps for chosen sources). Posterior-weighted,
     * RMS-normalised online gradient (regmem.relmem in the Python reference).
     */
    public static final class RelMem implements Model {
        final int K, S1, L, S, maxlag; final int[] lags; final int[][] sources; final double stay, lr, l2, gd, temper;
        final double[] b, Ab, W, AW, V, AV, A, a, pi, ll, E, g; final int[][] recent; final int[] tg, av; int seen;
        public RelMem(int K, int[] lags, int[][] sources, double stay, double lr, double l2, double gd, double temper, long seed, double init) {
            if (K < 1 || K > 4) throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_RELMEM_K");
            int ml = 0;
            for (int l : lags) { if (l < 1 || l > 5) throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_RELMEM_LAG"); ml = Math.max(ml, l); }
            for (int[] s : sources) { if (s[0] < 0 || s[0] > 9 || s[1] < 1 || s[1] > 5) throw new IllegalArgumentException("SU2_RELMEM_SOURCE"); ml = Math.max(ml, s[1]); }
            this.K = K; S1 = K + 1; this.lags = lags.clone(); L = lags.length; S = sources.length; maxlag = ml;
            this.sources = new int[S][]; for (int i = 0; i < S; i++) this.sources[i] = sources[i].clone();
            this.stay = stay; this.lr = lr; this.l2 = l2; this.gd = gd; this.temper = temper;
            double[] z = DeterministicRandomSU2.normals(seed, K * 100);
            b = new double[K * 100]; for (int i = 0; i < K * 100; i++) b[i] = init * z[i];
            Ab = new double[K * 100]; Arrays.fill(Ab, 1e-3);
            W = new double[K * 10 * L * 10 * 4]; AW = new double[W.length]; Arrays.fill(AW, 1e-3);
            V = new double[K * S * 1000]; AV = new double[V.length]; Arrays.fill(AV, 1e-3);
            A = new double[S1 * S1];
            for (int i = 0; i < S1; i++) for (int j = 0; j < S1; j++) A[i * S1 + j] = i == j ? stay : (1 - stay) / K;
            a = new double[S1]; Arrays.fill(a, 1.0 / S1); pi = new double[S1]; ll = new double[S1];
            E = new double[K * 100]; g = new double[K * 100];
            recent = new int[Math.max(1, maxlag)][10]; tg = new int[L * 40]; av = new int[S * 10];
        }
        public int stateWidth() { return 3; }
        private boolean ready() { return seen >= maxlag; }
        private int[] lagged(int lag) { return recent[(seen - lag) % maxlag]; }
        private void prior() { for (int j = 0; j < S1; j++) { double s = 0; for (int i = 0; i < S1; i++) s += a[i] * A[i * S1 + j]; pi[j] = s; } }
        private void emissions() {
            boolean r = ready();
            if (r) {
                for (int l = 0; l < L; l++) { int[] src = lagged(lags[l]); for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) { int s = src[q] - 1, o = (l * 10 + q) * 4; tg[o] = s; tg[o + 1] = (s + 1) % 10; tg[o + 2] = (s + 9) % 10; tg[o + 3] = 9 - s; } }
                for (int s = 0; s < S; s++) { int[] src = lagged(sources[s][1]); for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) av[s * 10 + p] = src[(p + sources[s][0]) % 10] - 1; }
            }
            double[] zz = new double[10];
            for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) zz[n] = b[k * 100 + p * 10 + n];
                if (r) {
                    double[] add = new double[10];
                    for (int l = 0; l < L; l++) for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) add[tg[(l * 10 + q) * 4 + f]] += W[(((k * 10 + p) * L + l) * 10 + q) * 4 + f];
                    if (L > 0) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) zz[n] += add[n];
                    if (S > 0) { double[] vs = new double[10]; for (int s = 0; s < S; s++) { int base = (((k * S + s) * 10 + p) * 10 + av[s * 10 + p]) * 10; for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) vs[n] += V[base + n]; } for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) zz[n] += vs[n]; }
                }
                double m = Double.NEGATIVE_INFINITY; for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) m = Math.max(m, zz[n]);
                for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] = Math.exp(zz[n] - m);
                double sum = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(E, k * 100 + p * 10, 10);
                for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] /= sum;
            }
        }
        public void predict(float[][] out, float[] st) {
            emissions(); prior();
            for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double v = 0; for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) v += pi[k + 1] * E[k * 100 + p * 10 + n]; out[p][n] = (float) (pi[0] * 0.1 + v); }
            regimeState(pi, K, st);
        }
        public void update(int[] d) {
            requireDraw(d);
            emissions(); prior();
            ll[0] = 10 * Math.log(0.1) * temper; double lmax = ll[0];
            double[] lg = new double[10];
            for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) { for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) lg[p] = Math.log(E[k * 100 + p * 10 + d[p] - 1]); ll[k + 1] = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(lg, 0, 10) * temper; lmax = Math.max(lmax, ll[k + 1]); }
            double z = 0; for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) { a[k] = pi[k] * Math.exp(ll[k] - lmax); z += a[k]; }
            for (int k = 0; k < S1; k++) a[k] /= z;
            for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { int i = k * 100 + p * 10 + n; g[i] = (E[i] - (n == d[p] - 1 ? 1.0 : 0.0)) * a[k + 1]; }
            for (int i = 0; i < K * 100; i++) { double gb = g[i] + l2 * b[i]; Ab[i] = gd * Ab[i] + gb * gb; b[i] -= lr * gb / Math.sqrt(Ab[i]); }
            if (ready()) {
                for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) for (int l = 0; l < L; l++) for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) for (int f = 0; f < 4; f++) {
                    int i = (((k * 10 + p) * L + l) * 10 + q) * 4 + f; double gw = g[k * 100 + p * 10 + tg[(l * 10 + q) * 4 + f]] + l2 * W[i];
                    AW[i] = gd * AW[i] + gw * gw; W[i] -= lr * gw / Math.sqrt(AW[i]);
                }
                for (int k = 0; k < K; k++) for (int s = 0; s < S; s++) for (int p = 0; p < 10; p++) {
                    int base = (((k * S + s) * 10 + p) * 10 + av[s * 10 + p]) * 10;
                    for (int n = 0; n < 10; n++) { double cur = V[base + n], gv = g[k * 100 + p * 10 + n] + l2 * cur; AV[base + n] = gd * AV[base + n] + gv * gv; V[base + n] = cur - lr * gv / Math.sqrt(AV[base + n]); }
                }
            }
            if (maxlag > 0) System.arraycopy(d, 0, recent[seen % maxlag], 0, 10);
            seen++;
        }
        public void write(DataOutput o) throws IOException {
            writeArr(o, b); writeArr(o, Ab); writeArr(o, W); writeArr(o, AW); writeArr(o, V); writeArr(o, AV); writeArr(o, a);
            o.writeInt(seen); for (int[] r : recent) for (int v : r) o.writeByte(v);
        }
        public void read(DataInput in) throws IOException {
            readArr(in, b); readArr(in, Ab); readArr(in, W); readArr(in, AW); readArr(in, V); readArr(in, AV); readArr(in, a);
            seen = in.readInt(); for (int[] r : recent) for (int i = 0; i < 10; i++) r[i] = in.readByte();
        }
    }
```

**验收**

- RegMemSU2Test：5 种配置成员文本与 Python 相同、概率表差 ≤7.5e-9（RELMEM 逐位相同）、t=2500 检查点续跑 0 差异；3 个策略 + 1 个委员会 5,000 期实时=回放 0 不一致（R16_REGMEM_PARITY_PASS）
- validation/r16：区制世界 R16 15% 层前推期望命中 ≥ R13 与 R15；无结构世界与打乱对照不报假；任何世界不比 R13 差 0.5 点以上（run_r13_checks L2b）

**回滚**：r16=false：不生成、不读取新族；已存 REGMEM/RELMEM 程序照常回放（模型类保留）。

#### P49 阶段与窗口层：阶段识别、原子延续/方向矩阵、观察—出手、择期出手（抓住阶段窗口，频率越多越好，质量不降）

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P48, P43, P45, P03；补丁 01-P05；覆盖要求 U37；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 用户要求：抓的是某一时间段的走势结构——每个算法公式有自己需要的走势结构，符合的位置进入观察，达到出手程度选最佳公式与位置；要能准确判断走势是否延续、是否转换、转换成什么；连续形态（如某位置连出十期大）之后下一期、下十期怎么走要有底层原子解释；频率 15–30% 以上越多越好。
- R16 之前这些只有零散部件（区制记忆、变点、教师矩阵、适用状态门），没有以“阶段”为中心的完整层；也没有每期更新的“形态出现后延续/转向”的统计。
- 原型实测（docs/17 §6e、§6h）：按策略近期命中追窗口（窗口引擎）比模型自身置信度差（区制世界 39.4% 对 42.5%）——命中只有 0/1，噪声大；“阶段 × 置信度”融合在区制世界相同频率下比最好的单家族高 1.3–1.9 点并能提高频率，但在动量/回补世界不如最好家族 → 必须带验证保护。

**做什么（目标）**

一个部署层（不改变进化与主链规则）：① 原子延续/方向矩阵 ContMatSU2：165 个原子的极端值、大/小/奇/偶连续 3/5/7/10 期、100/200 期偏少/偏多，按“全位置 + 每个位置”逐块计数“出现后号码是否到来”，前后两半同向显著（|z| ≥ 2.5 / 3.5）才算已证实，输出每期的延续/转向报告与概率表；② 阶段层 StageLayerSU2：窗口原子指纹 → 阶段库（高斯混合，BIC 选阶段数）→ 粘滞阶段后验 → 每个候选策略的“阶段 × 置信度”命中概率；③ 观察—出手：候选 = 三层委员会 + 名人堂前 8 + 延续矩阵，每期取预测命中最高者，在近 720 期该值的前 15% / 30% / 50% 出手；④ 保护：在检查点前的验证段上不如三层委员会就不启用（主链仍由 IPM 决定，永不劣于 XI）。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. Python 参考：contmat.py（流式，与 Java 逐位一致）、stagelayer_core.py（确定性：幂迭代主成分分位初始化、60 次 EM、IRLS 8 步）、stage_layer.py（在 rolling14 每个检查点保存的部署策略上评估，含验证保护）；研究原型 window_engine.py / stage_engine.py / observe_engine.py / multiperiod_scan.py / direction_scan.py / continuation_scan.py。
2. Java：ContMatSU2（OnlineModelsSU2.Model，成员种类 CONTMAT；块计数最多保留 40 块 = 2 万期，约 0.6 MB 状态）、StageLayerSU2（fingerprint/row、fit、apply、Live 每期一步；与 Python 差 ≤1e-13，实时=批量 0 差异）。
3. 接入：检查点（ModelFoundryJobSU2 每次活动结束）用已存开奖与候选回放结果 fit；实时车道每期 Live.step；联赛中以“·阶段层”标签与三层委员会并列冻结结算；只有其样本外记录在 IPM 下胜过主链才可能上主链。
4. 真实数据（docs/17 §6b–§6i）：两份真实开奖上延续矩阵各次重拟合 0 条已证实规律（不出手）；阶段层前推 30.5–31.5%、观察—出手 31.0–33.1%（不显著），与打乱对照同一区间；所有数字原样写入。

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/ContMatSU2.java` 第 124–138 行：

```java
    private void refit() {
        int end = (int) (t / BLOCK), first = Math.max(START / BLOCK, end - MAX_BLOCKS), nbk = end - first, h1 = first + nbk / 2;
        verPat.clear(); verLog.clear(); verRate.clear(); verCells.clear();
        for (int j = 0; j < nPat; j++) for (int sc = 0; sc < 11; sc++) {
            double n1 = 0, k1 = 0, n2 = 0, k2 = 0;
            for (int b = first; b < h1; b++) { n1 += blkN.get(b - blockBase)[j * 11 + sc]; k1 += blkK.get(b - blockBase)[j * 11 + sc]; }
            for (int b = h1; b < end; b++) { n2 += blkN.get(b - blockBase)[j * 11 + sc]; k2 += blkK.get(b - blockBase)[j * 11 + sc]; }
            if (n1 < MIN_N || n2 < MIN_N) continue;
            double z1 = (k1 / n1 - 0.1) / Math.sqrt(0.09 / n1), z2 = (k2 / n2 - 0.1) / Math.sqrt(0.09 / n2), zt = sc == 0 ? Z_ALL : Z_POS;
            if (z1 * z2 > 0 && Math.abs(z1) >= zt && Math.abs(z2) >= zt) {
                double r = (k1 + k2) / (n1 + n2);
                verPat.add(new int[]{j, sc}); verLog.add(Math.log(Math.max(r, 1e-3) / 0.1)); verRate.add(r); verCells.add((long) (n1 + n2));
            }
        }
    }
```

```java
    public static Layer fit(double[][] F, double[][] confs, boolean[][] hits, int a, int b) {
        int d = F[0].length; List<double[]> blocks = new ArrayList<>();
        for (int i = a; i <= b - BLOCK; i += BLOCK) { double[] m = new double[d]; for (int u = i; u < i + BLOCK; u++) for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) m[j] += F[u][j]; for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) m[j] /= BLOCK; blocks.add(m); }
        int nb = blocks.size(); double[] mu = new double[d], sd = new double[d];
        for (double[] m : blocks) for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) mu[j] += m[j]; for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) mu[j] /= nb;
        for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) { double s = 0; for (double[] m : blocks) { double e = m[j] - mu[j]; s += e * e; } sd[j] = Math.sqrt(s / nb) + 1e-9; }
        double[][] Z = new double[nb][d]; for (int i = 0; i < nb; i++) for (int j = 0; j < d; j++) Z[i][j] = (blocks.get(i)[j] - mu[j]) / sd[j];
        Gmm best = null;
        for (int K = 1; K <= KMAX; K++) { Gmm g = gmmFit(Z, K); if (best == null || g.bic < best.bic) best = g; }
        Layer tmp = new Layer(best, mu, sd, null, null);
        int K = best.K(), n = b - a; double[][] post = new double[n][];
        double[] at = new double[K]; Arrays.fill(at, 1.0 / K);
        for (int u = 0; u < n; u++) { at = stickyStep(at, post(best, tmp.z(F[a + u]))); post[u] = at; }
        double[][] W = new double[confs.length][];
        for (int s = 0; s < confs.length; s++) {
            double[][] X = new double[n][]; double[] y = new double[n];
            for (int u = 0; u < n; u++) { X[u] = design(post[u], logitConf(confs[s][a + u])); y[u] = hits[s][a + u] ? 1 : 0; }
            W[s] = logitFit(X, y);
        }
        return new Layer(best, mu, sd, W, post[n - 1]);
    }
```

**验收**

- ContMatSU2Test：12 次重拟合的已证实报告与 Python 完全相同、概率表与状态差 0、t=4100 检查点续跑一致（R16_CONTMAT_PARITY_PASS）
- StageLayerSU2Test：指纹相对差 ≤1e-13、阶段数与 BIC 相同、P(hit) 差 ≤5e-14、2,000 期最佳候选 0 分歧、实时=批量 0 差异（R16_STAGE_PARITY_PASS）
- validation/r16/*.stage.json：有阶段结构的世界上阶段层不劣于三层委员会（验证保护生效）；无结构与打乱对照不报假

**回滚**：不启用阶段层即无影响；ContMat 只作为候选，不进入进化。

### 批次 J · P50 检测报告 = 升级诊断书（实盘自动发现问题→原因→所需能力→升级做法→预计效果→影子实测）；体积与内存有界、重要内容完整保留

#### P50 检测报告 = 升级诊断书：实盘自动诊断“差在哪·缺什么能力·怎么升级·升级后到多少·怎么验证”，创造能力重点体检；体积与内存有界、重要内容完整保留

- 优先级 **P0**；依赖 P31, P33, P34, P42, P48, P49；补丁 11-P50；覆盖要求 U38, U39, U40；覆盖粗糙方案卡 —

**为什么（现状问题与证据）**

- 用户要求：检测报告要能反映到底如何升级、需要什么能力、怎么做才能高胜率高稳定高品质，要具体详细；软件实盘运行中会发现所有问题并找到解决办法；创造能力是重中之重，创造的算法公式如何优化升级、各项能力如何动态迭代、升级后能到什么胜率稳定品质都要说清楚；同时体积内存不能无限堆积、重要的东西必须完整保留。
- 以往的检测报告（5.4.3）是“全部表全部行”的数据堆积（单个 JSON 121 MB），既看不出问题，也不说怎么升级；P31–P34 只解决了报告本身的体积。
- 源码核查（P50 期间）：① 实时 HUB 检查点把所有校准环（每个 ≈1 MB）写进同一行，su2_live_state 的 CHECK(byte_count ≤ 16 MB) 在约 16 个校准成员时就会失败，而失败发生在结算事务内；② liveGenomes 按 genome_key 字母序取 32 个，最新冠军可能不在实时集合里，且成员数无上限（内存随联赛增长）；③ su2_live_daily、su2_mainline_binding（每期一行）、su2_mainline_choice、su2_certificate、旧的 su2_live_state 所有者没有寿命。

**做什么（目标）**

每 500 期自动生成一份《升级诊断书》（UPGRADE_DIAGNOSIS.md ≤64 KB + .json ≤256 KB，随检测报告导出）：一页结论（主链/XI/各层实测、距 35% 差多少、还要多少次出手才能证明、现有能力预计可达最高并做多重比较校正）→ 创造能力体检（逐代前推对照、算子成效与下一轮配比、家族成效×信息探针、原子分级、父子修复、选择衰减）→ 当前公式逐条诊断（教师矩阵：哪种状态下赢/输 → 加门或换成员）→ 结构探测（9 个家族信息探针、延续矩阵、阶段、区制）→ 诊断码（证据/原因/需要的能力/具体做法/升级后预计/验证）→ 12 项能力动态迭代表 → 影子处方实测 → 问题台账 → 存储与内存。所有存储有固定上限，重要状态以充分统计量完整保留。

**怎么做（设计）**

1. 信息探针 InformationProbeSU2（diagnosis_probe.py）：MSDIR/BOCPD/HMM/LOGLIN/RELBAYES/KALMAN/REGMEM/RELMEM/CONTMAT 各一个代表配置，每期 模型→在线校准→最佳位置前三→因果门（近 720 期 15%/30% 分位，INFORMATIVE 守卫：均匀表不出手）；只保留最近 40 个 500 期块的充分统计量（期数、对数似然增益和与平方和、两档门的出手/命中/预测命中、REGMEM 无结构后验）+ 终身累计。在突发任务里按 2 秒片段追赶，不进开奖前/结算路径。
2. 诊断引擎 UpgradeDiagnosisSU2（diagnosis.py）：快照文本（≤16 KB，唯一输入）→ 固定规则 22 条诊断码（G1–G5 目标/频率/稳定/主链；S1–S9 信息瓶颈/转化瓶颈/阶段依赖/延续/阶段层/区制；Q1–Q4 校准/选择衰减/层级倒挂/虚假原子；E1–E3 逐代/算子/修复；M1 存储）→ 预计 = 实测命中向 30% 经验贝叶斯收缩（κ=200）+ 90% 区间，“最高”做 Bonferroni 校正 → 文本单一来源 diagnosis_text.py 生成 DiagnosisTextSU2.java；数字格式按二进制精确值 half-even，Java 与 Python 逐字节一致。
3. 处方三类：SHADOW（ADD_MEMBER 家族成员、TIER_COVERAGE 频率）自动生成影子挑战者委员会进联赛冻结结算，满 600 次与基线同期配对判定 有效/无效/有害（无效与有害自动退役，有效者留给 IPM）；AUTO（算子配比、原子先验——进化引擎本来就按成效执行，诊断书把实际配比写出来）；MANUAL（校准上限、裁判 κ、阶段层启用条件等参数，下一轮施工执行）。问题台账跨报告合并：新发现/持续/改善中/已解决/回退，≤256 条（先淘汰已解决、再低优先级，当前 P0 永不淘汰）；逐代曲线 ≤512 点（抽稀保留首点、最近 64 点、最好点，权重与和精确）。
4. 体积与内存（DiagnosisStoreSU2 + 实时循环）：su2_track_block 每个成员最近 40 块 + 终身一行（总数精确）；su2_live_daily 90 天；su2_mainline_binding 10,080 期；su2_mainline_choice 保留每次换人 + 最近 2,000 行（任一期的主链成员不变）；证书过期 30 天后删除；不在联赛/名人堂的实时状态删除；诊断快照 8 份；HUB 检查点拆分为模型一行 + 每个校准环一行（每行 ≈1 MB，远低于 16 MB）；实时集合 = 当前主链成员 + 最新成员，最多 32 个策略、48 个校准成员（内存上限约 48 MB 校准环 + 模型）；StrategySchemaSU2 版本 R13_2，ready() 含新表（旧库下次自动建表）。
5. 实测（validation/r16/diagnosis，4 个数据集 16 份诊断书）：真实开奖 543 → 判定 D（全部 9 个家族探针前推增益不显著大于 0，最高 KALMAN 15% 门 33.3% 但预计 32.2%（29.0–35.4%），主链=XI 33.4%，诊断码 S1 信息瓶颈 + G1/Q2/Q3/S7…）；时间打乱对照 → 判定 D（不报假）；区制世界 → 判定 A（探针 REGMEM 预计 39.0%，校正后下界 36.6% > 35%，诊断码 S2·REGMEM/HMM 转化瓶颈 + Q1 校准）；跨位置世界 → 判定 B（探针 RELMEM 预计 34.8%，S2·RELMEM）。同一套规则在有结构时指出该加哪个家族、在无结构时如实说明瓶颈在信息而不是搜索。

**代码（从真实文件截取）**

`app/src/main/java/com/ethk3/novaonepro/strategyv2/UpgradeDiagnosisSU2.java` 第 245–413 行：

```java
    public static Diagnosis diagnose(Snapshot S) {
        Diagnosis D = new Diagnosis(); D.S = S;
        for (Map.Entry<String, Track> e : S.tracks.entrySet()) D.tracks.put(e.getKey(), trackSum(e.getValue()));
        for (Map.Entry<String, List<ProbeBlock>> e : S.probes.entrySet()) D.probes.put(e.getKey(), probeSum(e.getValue()));
        Map<String, TrackSum> TR = D.tracks; Map<String, ProbeSum> PR = D.probes;
        TrackSum main = TR.get("MAINLINE"), xi = TR.get("XI");
        String NA = T.get("NA");
        List<Cand> cands = new ArrayList<>();
        for (String t : new String[]{"F15", "F30", "F100", "XI"}) {
            TrackSum s = TR.get(t); if (s == null || s.acts == 0) continue;
            Cand c = new Cand(); c.name = t.equals("XI") ? "XI" : "冠军 " + t; c.k = s.hits; c.n = s.acts; c.cov = s.cov; c.worst = s.worst; c.c30 = s.cov != null && s.cov >= 0.30; cands.add(c);
        }
        for (Map.Entry<String, ProbeSum> e : PR.entrySet()) {
            ProbeSum p = e.getValue();
            for (int tag = 0; tag < 2; tag++) {
                int a = tag == 0 ? p.a15 : p.a30; if (a == 0) continue;
                Cand c = new Cand(); c.name = "探针 " + e.getKey() + " " + (tag == 0 ? "15" : "30") + "% 门"; c.k = tag == 0 ? p.h15 : p.h30; c.n = a;
                c.cov = tag == 0 ? p.cov15 : p.cov30; c.worst = tag == 0 ? p.worst15 : p.worst30; c.c30 = c.cov >= 0.30; cands.add(c);
            }
        }
        for (Stage st : S.stages) if (st.nl > 0 && st.fl != null) {
            Cand c = new Cand(); c.name = "阶段层 " + st.cov + " 档"; c.k = (int) Math.rint(st.fl * st.nl); c.n = st.nl; c.cov = Double.parseDouble(st.cov); c.c30 = c.cov >= 0.30; cands.add(c);
        }
        for (Cand c : cands) if (c.cov != null && c.cov >= 0.15) D.cands.add(c);
        Cand best = null;
        for (Cand c : D.cands) { double[] s = shrink(c.k, c.n); c.m = s[0]; c.sd = s[1]; if (best == null || c.m > best.m) best = c; }
        D.best = best;
        if (best != null) {
            best.adjlo = best.m - zk(D.cands.size()) * best.sd;
            D.verdict = best.adjlo > TARGET ? "A" : best.adjlo > CHANCE ? "B" : best.m - Z90 * best.sd > CHANCE ? "C" : "D";
        }
        List<Finding> F = D.findings;
        String bestProj = best != null ? projText(best.k, best.n) : NA, bestName = best != null ? best.name : NA;
        // ---- G
        if (main != null && main.acts > 0) {
            if (main.lcb < TARGET)
                F.add(new Finding("G1", main.rate < TARGET ? "P0" : "P1", "", main.rate, "", kv("rate", pct(main.rate), "hits", String.valueOf(main.hits), "acts", String.valueOf(main.acts),
                    "lcb", pct(main.lcb), "gap", gapPhrase(main.rate), "proj", bestProj, "best", bestName)));
            if (main.cov < 0.30) {
                Cand b30 = null; for (Cand c : D.cands) if (c.c30 && (b30 == null || c.m > b30.m)) b30 = c;
                F.add(new Finding("G2", main.cov < 0.15 ? "P0" : "P2", "", main.cov, "TIER_COVERAGE F15 0.30", kv("cov", pct(main.cov),
                    "proj", b30 != null ? projText(b30.k, b30.n) : NA, "best", b30 != null ? b30.name : NA, "cov30", b30 != null ? pct(b30.cov) : NA)));
            }
            if (main.range != null && (main.worst < 0.28 || main.range > 0.12)) {
                Cand stable = null; for (Cand c : D.cands) if (c.worst != null && (stable == null || c.worst > stable.worst)) stable = c;
                F.add(new Finding("G3", "P1", "", main.range, "STABILITY_PENALTY x1.5", kv("worst", pct(main.worst), "best", pct(main.best), "range", fx(main.range * 100.0, 1),
                    "nb", String.valueOf(main.nb), "stable", stable != null ? stable.name : NA, "sworst", stable != null ? pct(stable.worst) : NA)));
            }
        }
        if (S.xishare != null && xi != null && xi.acts > 0) {
            long on = S.xishare[0], tot = S.xishare[1]; double pw = S.pworse;
            if (pw < 0.05) F.add(new Finding("G5", "P0", "", pw, "", kv("pworse", fx(pw, 4), "xirate", pct(xi.rate))));
            else if (tot > 0 && (double) on / tot >= 0.95) {
                String bt = null;
                for (String t : new String[]{"F15", "F30"}) { TrackSum s = TR.get(t); if (s != null && s.acts > 0 && (bt == null || s.rate > TR.get(bt).rate)) bt = t; }
                if (bt != null && TR.get(bt).rate > xi.rate) {
                    Integer nd = need(TR.get(bt).rate, xi.rate);
                    F.add(new Finding("G4", "P2", "", (double) on / tot, "", kv("share", pct((double) on / tot), "tier", bt, "rate", pct(TR.get(bt).rate),
                        "acts", String.valueOf(TR.get(bt).acts), "xirate", pct(xi.rate), "need", nd != null ? String.valueOf(nd) : NA)));
                }
            }
        }
        // ---- S
        double kz = PR.isEmpty() ? Z90 : zk(2 * PR.size());
        for (Map.Entry<String, ProbeSum> e : PR.entrySet()) {
            ProbeSum p = e.getValue();
            if (p.lo != null && p.lo > 0 && p.gain >= MIN_GAIN) D.informative.add(e.getKey());
            else if (p.a15 >= 100 && p.pb15 != null && p.pb15 >= 0.6) { double[] s = shrink(p.h15, p.a15); if (s[0] - kz * s[1] > CHANCE) D.hint.add(e.getKey()); }
        }
        int[] cm = S.contmat;
        Integer stageK = S.stages.isEmpty() ? null : S.stages.get(0).k;
        if (!PR.isEmpty() && D.informative.isEmpty() && D.hint.isEmpty()) {
            String bg = bestGainProbe(PR), br = null;
            for (Map.Entry<String, ProbeSum> e : PR.entrySet()) { ProbeSum p = e.getValue(); if (p.a15 > 0 && (br == null || shrink(p.h15, p.a15)[0] > shrink(PR.get(br).h15, PR.get(br).a15)[0])) br = e.getKey(); }
            if (bg != null) {
                ProbeSum pg = PR.get(bg);
                F.add(new Finding("S1", "P0", "", pg.lo != null ? pg.lo : pg.gain, "", kv("n", String.valueOf(PR.size()), "fam", bg, "gain", snum(pg.gain, 3), "lo", snum(pg.lo, 3), "hi", snum(pg.hi, 3),
                    "rfam", br != null ? br : NA, "rate", br != null ? pct(PR.get(br).r15) : NA, "proj", br != null ? projText(PR.get(br).h15, PR.get(br).a15) : NA,
                    "cm", cm != null ? String.valueOf(cm[1]) : NA, "k", stageK != null ? String.valueOf(stageK) : NA)));
            }
        }
        for (String fam : PR.keySet()) {
            if (!D.informative.contains(fam) && !D.hint.contains(fam)) continue;
            ProbeSum p = PR.get(fam); double[] s = shrink(p.h15, p.a15);
            if (main == null || main.acts == 0 || s[0] > main.rate + 0.01) {
                String member = fam + "{" + S.params.getOrDefault(fam, "") + "}";
                F.add(new Finding("S2", "P0", fam, s[0], "ADD_MEMBER " + member, kv("fam", fam, "gain", snum(p.gain, 3), "lo", snum(p.lo, 3), "hi", snum(p.hi, 3),
                    "rate", pct(p.r15), "hits", String.valueOf(p.h15), "acts", String.valueOf(p.a15), "pb", pct(p.pb15), "adjlo", pct(s[0] - kz * s[1]),
                    "proj", projText(p.h15, p.a15), "main", main != null && main.acts > 0 ? pct(main.rate) : NA, "member", member, "worst", pct(p.worst15),
                    "cal", p.a15 > 0 ? pts(p.pred15 - p.r15) : NA)));
            }
        }
        List<String> s3 = new ArrayList<>();
        for (Map.Entry<String, ProbeSum> e : PR.entrySet()) {
            String fam = e.getKey(); ProbeSum p = e.getValue();
            if (D.informative.contains(fam) || D.hint.contains(fam) || p.gain == null || p.pos == null || p.gain < MIN_GAIN) continue;
            int nb = 0; for (ProbeBlock b : S.probes.get(fam)) if (b.n >= MIN_PROBE_BLOCK) nb++;
            if (nb >= 4 && 0.35 <= p.pos && p.pos <= 0.75) s3.add(fam);
        }
        s3.sort((a, b) -> Double.compare(-PR.get(a).gain, -PR.get(b).gain));
        for (String fam : s3.subList(0, Math.min(MAX_S3, s3.size()))) {
            ProbeSum p = PR.get(fam);
            F.add(new Finding("S3", "P1", fam, p.pos, "REGMEM_GATE " + fam, kv("fam", fam, "gain", snum(p.gain, 3), "pos", pct(p.pos), "proj", p.a15 > 0 ? projText(p.h15, p.a15) : NA)));
        }
        if (cm != null) {
            if (cm[2] == 0 && cm[0] >= 4) F.add(new Finding("S4", "P2", "", 0.0, "", kv("refits", String.valueOf(cm[0]))));
            else if (cm[1] > 0) {
                ProbeSum pc = PR.get("CONTMAT");
                F.add(new Finding("S5", "P1", "", (double) cm[1], "ADD_MEMBER CONTMAT{}", kv("n", String.valueOf(cm[1]), "patterns", S.contmatPatterns.isEmpty() ? NA : S.contmatPatterns,
                    "proj", pc != null && pc.a15 > 0 ? projText(pc.h15, pc.a15) : NA)));
            }
        }
        if (!S.stages.isEmpty()) {
            if (stageK != null && stageK <= 1) F.add(new Finding("S6", "P2", "", (double) stageK, "", kv("k", String.valueOf(stageK))));
            for (Stage st : S.stages) {
                if (st.use != 0 && st.fl != null && st.fc != null && st.nl >= MIN_BLOCK_ACTS) {
                    Map<String, String> v = kv("cov", st.cov, "vl", pct(st.vl), "vc", pct(st.vc), "fl", pct(st.fl), "nl", String.valueOf(st.nl), "fc", pct(st.fc), "nc", String.valueOf(st.nc));
                    if (st.fl < st.fc) F.add(new Finding("S7", "P1", st.cov, st.fc - st.fl, "STAGE_GUARD lead>=0.02 n>=150", v));
                    else if (st.fl > st.fc) { v.put("proj", projText((int) Math.rint(st.fl * st.nl), st.nl)); F.add(new Finding("S8", "INFO", st.cov, st.fl, "", v)); }
                }
    // … 其余 49 行见 strategyv2/UpgradeDiagnosisSU2.java
```

```java
    // ------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------ sufficient statistics
    static void foldTrackBlocks(SQLiteDatabase db, long seq) {
        long hi = (seq / BLOCK) * BLOCK, lo = Math.max(0, hi - (long) (KEEP_BLOCKS + 1) * BLOCK);
        db.execSQL("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO su2_track_block(member_id,block_index,periods,actions,hits,phat_sum,phat_n) "
            + "SELECT genome_key,sequence_ordinal/500,count(*),sum(act),sum(CASE WHEN act=1 THEN hit ELSE 0 END),coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN act=1 AND phat IS NOT NULL THEN phat END),0),"
            + "sum(CASE WHEN act=1 AND phat IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) FROM su2_live_decision WHERE hit IS NOT NULL AND sequence_ordinal>=? AND sequence_ordinal<? "
            + "GROUP BY genome_key,sequence_ordinal/500", new Object[]{lo, hi});
        db.execSQL("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO su2_track_block(member_id,block_index,periods,actions,hits,phat_sum,phat_n) "
            + "SELECT 'MAINLINE',d.sequence_ordinal/500,count(*),sum(d.act),sum(CASE WHEN d.act=1 THEN d.hit ELSE 0 END),coalesce(sum(CASE WHEN d.act=1 AND d.phat IS NOT NULL THEN d.phat END),0),"
            + "sum(CASE WHEN d.act=1 AND d.phat IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) FROM su2_live_decision d JOIN su2_league l ON l.genome_key=d.genome_key "
            + "WHERE d.hit IS NOT NULL AND d.sequence_ordinal>=? AND d.sequence_ordinal<? AND l.member_id=(SELECT c.member_id FROM su2_mainline_choice c WHERE c.from_sequence<=d.sequence_ordinal ORDER BY c.from_sequence DESC LIMIT 1) "
            + "GROUP BY d.sequence_ordinal/500", new Object[]{lo, hi});
        long cut = seq / BLOCK - KEEP_BLOCKS;              // blocks below cut leave into the lifetime row (exact totals)
        db.execSQL("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO su2_track_block(member_id,block_index,periods,actions,hits,phat_sum,phat_n) "
            + "SELECT DISTINCT member_id,-1,0,0,0,0,0 FROM su2_track_block WHERE block_index>=0 AND block_index<?", new Object[]{cut});
        db.execSQL("UPDATE su2_track_block SET "
            + "periods=periods+(SELECT coalesce(sum(b.periods),0) FROM su2_track_block b WHERE b.member_id=su2_track_block.member_id AND b.block_index>=0 AND b.block_index<?),"
            + "actions=actions+(SELECT coalesce(sum(b.actions),0) FROM su2_track_block b WHERE b.member_id=su2_track_block.member_id AND b.block_index>=0 AND b.block_index<?),"
            + "hits=hits+(SELECT coalesce(sum(b.hits),0) FROM su2_track_block b WHERE b.member_id=su2_track_block.member_id AND b.block_index>=0 AND b.block_index<?),"
            + "phat_sum=phat_sum+(SELECT coalesce(sum(b.phat_sum),0) FROM su2_track_block b WHERE b.member_id=su2_track_block.member_id AND b.block_index>=0 AND b.block_index<?),"
            + "phat_n=phat_n+(SELECT coalesce(sum(b.phat_n),0) FROM su2_track_block b WHERE b.member_id=su2_track_block.member_id AND b.block_index>=0 AND b.block_index<?) "
            + "WHERE block_index=-1", new Object[]{cut, cut, cut, cut, cut});
        db.execSQL("DELETE FROM su2_track_block WHERE block_index>=0 AND block_index<?", new Object[]{cut});
        // statistics of genomes that no longer exist at all (pruned research genomes) leave with them
        db.execSQL("DELETE FROM su2_track_block WHERE member_id<>'MAINLINE' AND member_id NOT IN (SELECT genome_key FROM su2_model_genome)");
    }
```

```java
    private void stepProbe(Probe p, int[] draw) {
        p.model.predict(p.raw, p.st);
        float[][] pc = p.cal.predict(p.raw);
        // picks: top-3 per position by (value desc, number asc), best position by mass (first maximum)
        int p1 = 0; double best = -1; int[] top = new int[3]; int[] bestTop = new int[3];
        for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) {
            top3(pc[q], top);
            double m = 0; m += pc[q][top[0]]; m += pc[q][top[1]]; m += pc[q][top[2]];
            if (m > best) { best = m; p1 = q; System.arraycopy(top, 0, bestTop, 0, 3); }
        }
        double conf = best;
        if (t >= START && (t - START) % STEP == 0) {           // refresh the causal gate from the last 720 confidences
            if (p.ringN >= MIN_HISTORY) { p.thr15 = quantile(p, 1 - 0.15); p.thr30 = quantile(p, 1 - 0.30); }
            else { p.thr15 = Double.NaN; p.thr30 = Double.NaN; }
        }
        if (t >= START) {
            int bi = (int) (t / BLOCK);
            if (bi != p.openIndex) { closeBlock(p); p.openIndex = bi; }
            int x = draw[p1] - 1; boolean hit = bestTop[0] == x || bestTop[1] == x || bestTop[2] == x;
            double[] l = new double[10];
            for (int q = 0; q < 10; q++) l[q] = Math.log(Math.min(1.0, Math.max(1e-9, (double) pc[q][draw[q] - 1])) / 0.1);
            p.bLlr[p.bn] = ModelOpsSU2.pairwise(l, 0, 10);
            p.bSt0[p.bn] = p.family.equals("REGMEM") ? (double) p.st[0] : Double.NaN;
            boolean inf = conf > INFORMATIVE;
            if (inf && !Double.isNaN(p.thr15) && conf >= p.thr15) { p.bP15[p.ba15++] = conf; if (hit) p.bh15++; }
            if (inf && !Double.isNaN(p.thr30) && conf >= p.thr30) { p.bP30[p.ba30++] = conf; if (hit) p.bh30++; }
            p.bn++;
        }
        p.ring[p.ringN < PAST ? p.ringN : (int) (t % PAST)] = conf;          // ring holds the last 720 confidences
        if (p.ringN < PAST) p.ringN++;
        p.cal.settle(p.raw, draw);
        p.model.update(draw);
    }
```

**验收**

- UpgradeDiagnosisSU2Test：4 个数据集 16 份诊断书的 .md/.json 与 Python 逐字节相同、问题台账文本相同；5,000 次诊断 × 600 个问题键 → 台账 ≤256 条、当前 P0 永不淘汰；10,000 次活动 → 逐代曲线 ≤512 点、首点/最近 64 点/最好点保留、权重与和精确；快照 8 份各 ≤16 KB；超大报告截断到 ≤64 KB 并注明（R16_DIAG_PARITY_PASS）
- InformationProbeSU2Test：真实开奖 4,995 期 9 个探针 63 个块，出手/命中逐个相同，对数似然与预测和相对差 ≤1e-9；t=3,210 检查点续跑一致（R16_PROBE_PARITY_PASS）
- test_su2_sql：3 个策略 + 主链 × 30,000 期 → 每成员 ≤41 行、终身总数与原始逐期完全相同；主链切换日志压缩后任一期的主链成员不变；过期实时状态与旧校准行被删；快照 >16 KB 被拒绝
- 设备上（L4）：每 500 期 UPGRADE_DIAGNOSIS 更新；检测报告含 UPGRADE_DIAGNOSIS.md/.json；30 天压力下 SU2 表与实时状态字节不随时间增长

**回滚**：不调用 DiagnosisStoreSU2.maintain 即无诊断（报告里写“尚无诊断”）；新表可留空；实时集合上限与 HUB 拆分可回退到单行写入（旧检查点不可读时自动从历史重建）。

## 6. 要求台账（40 条）

| 编号 | 要求 | 落实项目 | 怎样证明 |
|---|---|---|---|
| U01 | 升级后不得劣于旧版（绝对禁止） | P03, P02, P30, P35 | IPM：主链底线 = 用户原方法 XI；9 个数据集上主链从未显著差于 XI |
| U02 | 高胜率、高稳定、高品质 | P19, P25, P26, P27 | 稳定性适应度（折间极差惩罚）+ 选择性证书（下界>30%、四块≥28%） |
| U03 | 出手频率：最低 15%，希望 ≥30%，越多越好；很强才 100% | P25, P26, P03 | F15/F30/F100 三层冠军；IPM 频率底线 15% |
| U04 | 查清旧版高胜率算法是否真实（35/40% 从哪来） | P02 | 三级证据 + 同口径复现（docs/14 §1） |
| U05 | 查清“已能创造 35% 算法但主链不出手”的原因并修复 | P04, P27, P30 | 编译期常量锁/绝对门槛/全覆盖证书三处根因与补丁 |
| U06 | 算法必须是有底层逻辑、能计算整体状态走势的数学公式，不是冷热遗漏/小规律/调参 | P11, P12, P13, P14, P15, P16, P17, P18, P36, P39 | 六族在线概率模型 + 三种融合 + 可读公式；R14 在线校准；R14b 原子网络（原子是输入，权重在线学习） |
| U07 | 每期都计算、训练、结算、反馈（不出手的期也算） | P06, P08, P09 | live == replay；GATE_SKIP 也保留拟出手并结算 |
| U08 | 核查多位置多号码交叉交集的算法，作为对照并要被超越 | P03, P19 | XI 完整移植（0 差异）+ 在任者；META 门把“挑期”推广到所有策略 |
| U09 | 创造能力多元化：多维度、多尺度、多家族，不是一个思路调参 | P10, P20, P21, P39, P41 | 成员种类 × 融合 × 门 × 频率的开放空间 + 结构算子；R14b 原子网络组合空间（138 原子 × 交叉项 × 组/关系原子）+ 开放关系搜索 |
| U10 | 原子世界：每个号码/每期/各指标的底层关系与因果，成为可计算素材 | P10, P11, P12, P39 | 原子/SU1 程序作成员；跨位置关系可靠度图；条件因果图；R14b：Java 原子流逐位等于 Python，原子网络成为手机端可进化成员 |
| U11 | 迭代进化变异融合有效，一代比一代强 | P21, P23, P24, P37, P41, P48 | 有效代只在证实改进时+1；委员会融合；R14b 贡献引导子代；逐代曲线如实报告（docs/17） |
| U12 | 优秀父代不丢弃，通过反馈分析让子代更强 | P22, P23, P41 | 名人堂/谱系/跨活动延续；diagnose 定向修复；R14b 原子贡献反馈 |
| U13 | 保留历史数据快速训练回放 | P05, P07 | 4,995 期每族 <1 s；后台连续创造 |
| U14 | 实时开奖每一期也要训练结算反馈 | P06 | settleWithin 在结算事务内训练全部策略 |
| U15 | 保留以前所有有用的要求与思路 | P01 | 台账映射：本轮 24 条、粗糙 35 卡、R10–R12；旧 1832 条保留身份映射 |
| U16 | 分析出其他需要的能力功能并补上 | P03, P17, P19, P28, P29, P33, P36, P38, P40, P42 | 新增：IPM 样本外主链、状态门控融合、适用状态学习、终身 α、选择后校准、表寿命；R14 校准/裁判；R14b 经验贝叶斯裁判、留出世界 |
| U17 | 统合本对话分析、旧方案（299/35）、公共仓库方案、两仓最新源码 | P01 | docs/14 源码核查（带文件:行号） |
| U18 | 每个项目：编号、做什么、为什么、怎么做、几个文件、哪些文件、修改/删除/重写、代码 | P01 | docs/13 每项卡片 + 文件表 + 代码摘录 + 补丁 |

粗糙方案自列的 5 个缺口：

| 缺口 | 内容 | 项目 | 本轮状态 |
|---|---|---|---|
| G01 | 旧 152 期赛前账本与原输入 | P02 | OPEN（无法关闭）：库里只有展示记录，没有逐期冻结号码与原输入；按 LEGACY_REPORTED 保留，同时用同口径复现说明 35–40% 的来源。 |
| G02 | 逐文件完整 Android AFTER | P01/P35 | 大部分关闭：15 个 MODIFY 文件给出 BEFORE SHA-256 + 精确锚点补丁，10 个补丁在 0f1fb606 上按序 git am 通过；补丁后 29 个 SU2 类对真实 SU1 源码符号级编译通过。未关闭部分：Gradle assembleRelease（无 Android SDK）。 |
| G03 | 旧需求逐项语义矩阵 | P01 | 部分关闭：本轮 24 条要求 + 粗糙方案 35 卡 + R10–R12 逐条映射到项目与验收；旧 1832 条需求未逐条语义复核（只保留身份映射），列为施工前 P01 的人工复核项。 |
| G04 | 真机存储与延迟 | P31–P34 | 根因已在源码中定位并修复、宿主测试复现（20 次失败导出：旧 5.44 GB → 新 12 KB）；真机 30 天压力与 P95 延迟属 L4，未执行。 |
| G05 | 独立自然效果 | P27/P35 | OPEN（L5）：真实数据上没有任何策略在样本外达到证书标准；主链由 IPM 保证在同一批期次上不显著差于旧方法；真实效果只能由上线后的冻结前推证明。 |
